[1] alkhder at ImageEval 2026 Shared Tasks: Constrained Selection and Rotation-Averaged Logit Scoring for Multimodal Hallucination Detection cs.CLPDF

Maher Pasa, Maria Hanini, Amro Najjar, Hasan Alkhder

TL;DR: 该参赛系统用零样本7B视觉语言模型和候选轮换，处理英阿双语图像陈述真假选择任务，并降低了相对官方3B基线的CI分数。

Details

Motivation: ImageEval 2026子任务要求模型从配图的三个候选陈述中选出唯一视觉上成立的一项，且需同时应对英语与现代标准阿拉伯语。答案位置偏差可能影响选择结果，参赛方案希望在不针对任务微调的条件下控制此类偏差。

Result: 系统使用预训练7B视觉语言模型，在零样本设置下依据下一token对候选答案的对数概率作受约束选择，并以拉丁方方式轮换候选陈述的位置。英语测试集CI为0.0510，现代标准阿拉伯语测试集CI为0.0960；相较官方报告的3B模型devtest基线，CI分别降低83.7%和80.4%。

Insight: 结果显示，候选位置轮换与对数概率选择可在该共享任务设置中取得较低CI，且阿拉伯语表现相对基线的降幅也较大。比较涉及测试集与devtest基线，摘要未提供绝对准确率、置信区间或完整可复现配置，不能将CI降幅直接视为准确率提升。

Abstract: We describe our submission to Subtask~1b of ImageEval 2026. The task consists of selecting the single visually valid statement from three candidates paired with an image, and is in both English and Modern Standard Arabic (MSA). Our system uses a pre-trained 7B vision–language model in a zero-shot setting, without task-specific training or fine-tuning. We formulate the task as a constrained selection problem and choose the answer using next-token log-probabilities. To reduce answer-position bias, we apply a Latin-square rotation to the candidate statements. Our system achieves CI scores of 0.0510 on the English test set and 0.0960 on the MSA test set, reducing CI relative to the official 3B baselines reported on devtest by 83.7% and 80.4%, respectively.

[2] RealCLI: Toward a Unified Evaluation of Human-Agent Collaboration for CLI Agents cs.CLPDF

Yunqi Liu, Peiyu Wang, Bingli Wang, Leo Luo, Xuanwu Song

TL;DR: REALCLI用成对任务基准同时评估命令行智能体的执行效果与运行时人机协作，发现前沿智能体在可操控性、信任校准和安全边界方面仍较弱。

Details

Motivation: 现有CLI智能体基准通常假设用户一开始就给出完整且不变的指令，或只评价最终任务结果，因而没有衡量执行期间逐步补充信息、纠正方向、修改约束和授权决策等协作质量。

Result: REALCLI建立五维协作评估框架，包含22个子维度和51种交互场景模板。它提供配对模式：REALCLI-EXEC在开始时给出完整任务说明，REALCLI-COLLAB则由脚本化用户逐步提供信息、纠正、调整约束和授权决定。实验中最佳配置平均得分仅49.2%；联合协作分数J_collab最高为37.6%。模型在可操控性、信任校准和安全边界管理上反复失误。

Insight: 配对评测能揭示单看任务完成率时容易忽略的协作问题，可为CLI智能体的交互能力改进提供可复现基础。摘要没有给出具体模型清单及各子维度分数，报告的结果应理解为该基准设置下的总体表现。

Abstract: Command-line interface (CLI) agents increasingly execute longer-horizon tasks, yet existing benchmarks either assume that user instructions remain fixed throughout execution or evaluate task outcomes without capturing runtime collaboration quality. In this paper, we present REALCLI, a paired benchmark that unifies the evaluation of task execution and human-agent collaboration quality on realistic CLI tasks. To comprehensively measure collaboration, REALCLI builds a five-dimensional framework, operationalized into 22 sub-dimensions and 51 interaction scenario templates. REALCLI provides two modes for paired comparison: REALCLI-EXEC supplies complete specifications upfront, whereas REALCLI-COLLAB introduces a scripted user that delivers progressive information, corrections, constraint revisions, and authorization decisions. Evaluations reveal profound capability gaps in frontier agents: the best configuration attains an average score of only 49.2%, and when evaluated by the joint collaboration score ($J_{\mathrm{collab}}$), the peak performance reaches merely 37.6%. These results show that task effectiveness diverges sharply from collaboration quality, with models consistently struggling in steerability, trust calibration, and safety boundary management. REALCLI provides a rigorous, reproducible foundation for measuring and advancing collaborative CLI agents.

[3] A Plausible Explanation Is Enough to Mislead Multimodal Judges cs.CL | cs.AIPDF

Anglin Cai, Zhiqi Wang, Xiaozhi Yang, Yurun Lin, Dongwon Lee

TL;DR: JUPE研究多模态模型作为评审时的解释偏置：即使只是听起来合理的解释，也会让评审更容易接受错误答案。

Details

Motivation: 多模态大模型常在训练与评估流程中兼任答案评审，且通常同时接收答案和解释。文字线索会影响AI评审，但普通且貌似合理的解释是否会干扰必须通过图像核验的判断，仍不清楚。

Result: 作者提出JUPE视觉问答式基准，在固定答案的同时改变其解释，并评测14个多模态评审模型。普通合理解释会误导评审，平均影响与刻意说服性论证相当。简单理由和链式思维解释分别使错误答案被拒绝的比例降低12.7和13.8个百分点。基于信号检测理论，变化主要体现为决策标准偏向接受答案，平均辨别力没有明显变化。解释影响跨领域、解释生成器和评审流程均存在；测试的缓解方法效果有限且依赖模型。

Insight: 模型评审可能把解释的合理性误当成视觉证据，进而高估系统表现；评估设计应单独检查无依据的解释影响。摘要指出所测缓解方案并不稳定，且未提供具体模型与各领域差异，结论应限于该基准。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) increasingly serve as judges in training and evaluation pipelines, often assessing answers together with explanations. Although AI judges are sensitive to textual factors, it remains unclear whether ordinary plausible explanations can sway judgments requiring visual verification. We introduce JUPE, a visual-question-answering (VQA)-style benchmark that fixes answers while varying explanations. Across 14 MLLM judges, ordinary explanations that sound plausible mislead judges, with effects comparable on average to deliberately persuasive arguments. Plain justifications and chain-of-thought rationales reduce rejection of incorrect answers by 12.7 and 13.8 percentage points, respectively. We call this failure plausibility capture. Using signal detection theory, we find a significant mean shift in decision criterion toward acceptance, without a clear change in mean discriminability. Explanations sway judgments across domains, explanation generators, and judging protocols, while tested mitigations offer limited, model-dependent protection. Such shifts can inflate performance estimates when judges evaluate answers with explanations.

[4] ProPerAgent: Toward Memory-Driven Proactive Assistance cs.CLPDF

Shenglai Zeng, Xiao Yang, Tiffany Ma, Xinyuan Zhang, Nanshu Wang

TL;DR: ProPerMem基准评测智能体能否从长期个人记忆中判断何时主动提供帮助；ProPerAgent利用图组织记忆和触发控制，在两个模型上提高严格准确率。

Details

Motivation: 主动助手不只要从记忆里找到相关事实，还要判断是否以及何时打断用户。以往设定常把关键事实直接放入上下文；现实难点是从长期、含干扰信息的个人记忆中挖出支持介入的证据，并把握触发时机。

Result: ProPerMem将第一人称视频与平均约61.8万token、覆盖一年且含干扰的用户记忆配对，提供人工核验的触发时间窗，并联合评分内容与时机。所有基线严格准确率均不超过15%，包括使用摘要或检索增强记忆的前沿模型。受控实验中，GPT-5.3-Chat获得触发窗内的正确证据时达到96%；让模型自行选时机后降至38%，再用检索替代预言机证据后降至15%。ProPerAgent用图式记忆存储和中断门控决定何时提示，并用轻量强化学习减少过早和重复触发。未经训练时，Qwen3.8-27B和GPT-5.3-Chat严格准确率分别提升至37.5%和38%；强化学习后达到42.4%。

Insight: 实验表明，主动帮助同时依赖证据检索与时机选择，任一环节缺失都会造成大幅退化。ProPerAgent改善明显但最高报告准确率仍为42.4%；摘要未说明数据构建和部署隐私细节，不能据此视为已适合真实个人助手上线。

Abstract: As assistants move from reactive answering toward proactive intervention, memory changes role: it no longer only supplies the answer to a query, it determines whether and when to speak at all. Prior work in this setting hands the system the relevant fact in context; we study the regime that makes the problem hard, where the evidence justifying an intervention must be mined from a long-horizon personal memory. We introduce ProPerMem, a benchmark for memory-driven proactive assistance (MPA), pairing egocentric video with year-long, distractor-rich user memories (~618K tokens on average), human-verified trigger windows, and a protocol that scores content and timing jointly. Every baseline we evaluate performs poorly (at most 15%), including frontier models given summarized or retrieval-augmented memory. A controlled study explains why: with oracle evidence delivered inside the trigger window GPT-5.3-Chat reaches 96%; letting it choose the timing drops it to 38%, and replacing oracle evidence with retrieval drops it to 15%. Content and timing are separately necessary, and missing either is catastrophic. Guided by this, we build ProPerAgent (Proactive Personalized Agent) with a plug-and-play harness that surfaces memory from a graph-organized store and gates it down to what is worth interrupting for, plus a lightweight RL stage that curbs early and repeated firing. Without any training, ProPerAgent lifts strict accuracy from below 10% to 37.5% on Qwen3.8-27B and 38% on GPT-5.3-Chat; RL post-training reaches 42.4%.

[5] Multimodal Filesystem Memory For Long-Horizon Embodied Agents cs.CLPDF

Chloe Su, Jiahan Li, Zhenting Qi, Chaoran Cheng, Haoyu Chen

TL;DR: MFM为长时程具身智能体设计多模态文件系统记忆，连接经历、空间、语义、程序和当前计划，并在Minecraft与电脑操作任务上超过持久记忆基线。

Details

Motivation: 长期智能体若保留全部交互历史，查找相关经历会变困难；若压缩成摘要，又可能丢失后续任务需要的视觉和空间细节。因此需要一种既可检索抽象经验、又能回到原始多模态观察的记忆组织方式。

Result: MFM把情景记忆（观察、动作和结果）、空间记忆、语义与程序记忆、工作记忆分别组织起来，通过可搜索文本记录关联到底层多模态经历；智能体可按上下文检索旧经验并查看原始观察，巩固过程则将经历提炼为知识和可复用操作，同时保留原经历引用。三个基础模型在长时程Minecraft与电脑使用任务上均有提升。GPT-6-Astra可从零完成Mine Diamond from Scratch，在Ender Dragon任务上达到24个里程碑中的16个，高于最佳基线的12.7；在CUABench-KiCad上平均奖励0.440，优于最佳基线0.400及无持久记忆的0.267。

Insight: 该架构试图兼顾长期经验复用与原始视觉细节回溯，适用于历史记录长且任务反复出现的具身或桌面操作任务。摘要没有给出其他骨干的量化结果和存储开销，具体效果需结合任务与模型解释。

Abstract: Agents operating over long horizons need an effective memory that allows them to reuse their experience throughout long interactions. If the entire history of an interaction is retained, it becomes difficult to find the relevant experience, while compressing it into summaries may result in the loss of visual and spatial details that are required later on. We present \textbf{MFM}, a multimodal filesystem memory architecture that keeps complementary types of agent memory. \textbf{Episodic memory} stores the observations, actions, and outcomes, and \textbf{spatial memory} associates experiences with locations. \textbf{Semantic and procedural memory} build up general knowledge and reusable methods of acting, and \textbf{working memory} holds the current plans. MFM makes use of searchable textual records to connect these different representations to the underlying multimodal episodes. The agents are able to find previous experiences by means of contextual cues and to examine the original observations in order to recover details that are missing from their textual descriptions. Through consolidation, the agents convert experience into higher-level knowledge and procedures while still keeping references to the original episodes. In the case of long-horizon Minecraft and computer-use tasks, MFM leads to improved performance with three base models. With GPT-6-Astra, MFM completes \emph{Mine Diamond from Scratch} and reaches 16 out of 24 milestones on the \emph{Ender Dragon} task, as against 12.7 for the best baseline. On CUABench-KiCad it attains a mean reward of 0.440, as compared to 0.400 for the best baseline and 0.267 in the case of no persistent memory.

[6] Explore Separately, Adopt Selectively: Execution-Guided Tool Use over Large Tool Libraries cs.CL | cs.AIPDF

Anna Mazhar, Seiji Maekawa, Nikita Bhutani

TL;DR: BRIA通过把候选工具的检索和执行放在独立分支中检查，再让任务智能体只采纳有用证据，在大型工具库任务上达到或超过检索基线。

Details

Motivation: 语言模型智能体可能要从数千个工具中发现适合当前执行阶段的工具；当多个候选看起来都能完成任务时，往往必须实际调用才能辨别。若在同一轨迹中试错，未测试选项可能被忽略，失败调用也可能干扰后续推理。

Result: BRIA将候选工具调查放入互相隔离的临时分支：每个分支检索并执行候选，主任务智能体查看执行证据，只把有用结果并入当前轨迹；任务出现新能力需求时再检索工具。作者在PlanBench-XL和MCP-Atlas上使用1,897个共享工具、三种语言模型及静态和自适应检索基线进行比较。所有设置下BRIA均达到或超过最强检索基线；在MCP-Atlas上平均claim score为89.77，相比MCP-Zero的75.40提高14.37个百分点，同时处在任务表现与输入token成本的帕累托前沿。

Insight: 将探索性试调用与主任务推理隔离，有助于从执行结果判断工具价值而不污染连续轨迹。摘要未给出其他基准的详细分数、调用成本绝对值及分支并行开销，结论应限于测试工具库和任务。

Abstract: Large language model agents must discover relevant tools from large libraries, e.g., thousands of tools, as new needs arise during task execution. When several candidates could satisfy the same need, execution may be required to identify the relevant one. Investigating candidates in a shared trajectory can leave alternatives unexplored or allow failed attempts to influence subsequent reasoning. To address this, we present BRIA (BRanch-Inspect-Adopt), a simple yet effective agent that temporarily isolates candidate investigations in separate branches. Each branch retrieves and executes tools to investigate its candidate; a task agent inspects the resulting execution evidence and adopts only useful results into its continuing trajectory. BRIA retrieves additional tools as new capability needs arise during task execution. We evaluate BRIA on PlanBench-XL and MCP-Atlas using a shared pool of 1897 tools, three language models, and static and adaptive retrieval baselines. Across all settings, BRIA matches or exceeds the strongest retrieval baseline. On MCP-Atlas, it achieves an average claim score of 89.77, compared with 75.40 for MCP-Zero, a gain of 14.37 percentage points. BRIA also lies on the Pareto frontier for task performance and input-token cost.

[7] ARBOR: Locating Reasoning Failures in Long Chains of Thought through Reasoning State Tracking cs.CL | cs.AIPDF

Huihan Li, Siyuan Wang, Naga Vamsi Ramana Dinavahi, Xiaohui Liu, Xiang Ren

TL;DR: ARBOR用跨步骤推理状态树定位长思维链中的错误，将失败归因于某一步与此前建立状态的关系，并细分出不同类型的推理失误。

Details

Motivation: 过度思考、思考不足和无依据推断等问题，通常通过邻近文本相似度、切换token或模型对单步正确性的判断来检测。但一个步骤是否有问题取决于此前已经确立的推理状态，局部信号难以提供完整上下文。

Result: ARBOR把推理轨迹表示为状态树，记录每一步如何创建、验证、剪枝或重新访问状态，再将错误归因到当前步骤与前序状态之间的关系。基于这一表示，作者将三类既有失误细分为通过不同机制、付出不同代价损害推理的子类型。实验发现，在步骤之前维护的状态树能够区分实际失败步骤与存在风险但未发生失败的步骤。论文还讨论了将该框架用于推理时干预和训练数据改进。

Insight: 状态跟踪提供了对长推理轨迹更全局的错误分析视角，也区分了潜在风险和已发生失败。摘要未列出检测准确率、具体子类型定义和干预效果量化，不能据此断言ARBOR已提升最终任务正确率。

Abstract: Large reasoning models solve complex problems through long chains of thought, in which prior work has documented failure modes such as overthinking, underthinking, and unsupported deductions. Existing methods detect these failures from local signals: textual similarity to nearby steps, switch tokens, or an LLM’s judgment of a step’s correctness. However, what a step contributes to the solution depends on the state that earlier steps have established, so identifying failures requires a global view that tracks that state across the trajectory. Therefore, we propose ARBOR, a reasoning-failure attribution framework that represents each trajectory as a tree of reasoning states, built as each step creates, verifies, prunes, or revisits a state, and attributes each failure to a relation between a step and the state preceding it. Using ARBOR, we refine the three failure modes into distinct subtypes that harm reasoning through different mechanisms and at different costs. We find that the tree state ARBOR maintains before a step separates failure steps from the at-risk steps at which the failure did not occur. We discuss how ARBOR integrates with existing mitigations at inference time and in training data. Code, data and interactive examples are available at https://inklab.usc.edu/ARBOR.

[8] Self-Evolving Coding Agents via Harness-Policy Optimization cs.CL | cs.AIPDF

Zhepei Wei, Wenlin Yao, Baolin Peng, Danqing Wang, Qianhui Wu

TL;DR: Self-HPO交替优化代码智能体的执行框架和策略模型，让模型根据自身错误提出框架候选并在选定框架下强化学习，提升多个代码基准上的解决率。

Details

Motivation: 代码智能体表现由策略模型和连接环境的执行框架共同决定，两者相互影响。只调优固定策略的框架可能不适配更新后的模型，只训练策略也受限于固定框架所产生的轨迹。

Result: Self-HPO递归交替进行框架优化和策略优化：当前策略先分析自身失败并提出框架候选，随后在选择的框架下进行强化学习；训练中发现的难题作为下一轮框架搜索集合，使搜索聚焦于不断变化的失败模式。三个代码基准上，该方法稳定超过只优化框架或只优化策略的基线。Qwen3.5-35B-A3B平均解决率从57.8%提高到64.8%，且收益迁移到分布外设置。两种优化组件单独配合基础版本也保留部分收益，联合收益超过两者单独增益之和；Pass@1可靠性和Pass@k能力前沿均提升。使用更强的外部模型Opus 4.8提出框架还可继续提升表现。

Insight: 该框架把智能体工具与工作流设计纳入模型自我改进循环，并报告两者协同效应。摘要未列出三个基准名称、具体训练成本和框架修改范围；外部强模型能进一步提升也意味着效果可能依赖额外模型资源。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents have shown promising potential in coding tasks, and their performance is jointly determined by the policy model and the harness that mediates their interaction with the environment. Indeed, harness and policy are mutually reinforcing and evolving only one component is suboptimal—a harness tuned for one checkpoint may not target the failure modes of an updated policy, while policy learning remains limited to the trajectories elicited by a fixed harness. In light of this, we propose Self-HPO, a recursive self-improving framework for coding agents that alternates harness and policy optimization, using the evolving policy as its own harness proposer. Specifically, in each round, the current policy first analyzes its own failures and proposes new harness candidates (H-step). The policy is then updated through reinforcement learning (RL) under the selected harness (P-step), and hard tasks identified during training form the next harness search set, keeping harness optimization targeted to the policy’s evolving failure modes. Across three coding benchmarks, Self-HPO consistently outperforms harness-only and policy-only baselines, improving Qwen3.5-35B-A3B’s average resolve rate from 57.8% to 64.8%, with gains generalizing to out-of-distribution settings. Further analysis shows that both optimized components retain gains even when paired with their base counterparts, while their joint gain exceeds the sum of their individual gains. Self-HPO also improves reliability (Pass@1) and expands the capability frontier (Pass@k) over the base agent. Additionally, we show that with a stronger external model (e.g., Opus 4.8) as the harness proposer, the same framework yields a further performance boost.

[9] On the Position Bias of Large Language Models: A Comprehensive Study cs.CLPDF

Yining Li, Huayang Li, Lemao Liu, Taro Watanabe

TL;DR: 该研究跨模型规模、五种语言及选择题和多文档任务系统考察大语言模型的位置偏差，报告模型规模扩大和标准思维链提示可缓解偏差。

Details

Motivation: 答案或文档所在位置会影响语言模型选择，削弱选择题评测和检索增强生成的可靠性。已有研究模型覆盖较少且偏重英语，因此前沿模型和多语言中的偏差情况尚未充分了解。

Result: 作者分析从十亿到万亿参数规模的模型，并覆盖五种语言的选择题及多文档任务。实验发现，小规模模型和非英语任务中的位置偏差仍较明显；模型规模增大和采用标准链式思维提示可显著缓解。作者还发现，具备扩展推理能力的新型思考模型几乎不受位置偏差影响，并指出这是一种成本较高但可能通用的缓解方式。

Insight: 位置偏差会受模型能力、语言和提示方法影响，单一英语基准不足以全面评估。摘要没有给出模型清单、参数规模对应曲线或不同语言的具体偏差数值，“几乎免疫”限于作者测试的任务和设置。

Abstract: Recent studies have shown that position bias remains a critical vulnerability in large language models (LLMs), undermining both the integrity of multiple-choice evaluations and the reliability of retrieval-augmented generation. However, existing understanding of this phenomenon is largely derived from studies with limited model coverage and English-centric benchmarks, leaving the behavior of frontier models and diverse languages underexplored. In this work, we conduct a comprehensive investigation of position bias across model scales ranging from billion-level to trillion-level parameters, covering both multiple-choice and multi-document tasks in five languages. Our experiments reveal that while position bias remains noticeable in small-scale models and Non-English languages, increasing model scale and employing standard Chain-of-Thought prompting significantly mitigate this issue. Moreover, we demonstrate that emerging \emph{thinking models} with extended reasoning capabilities are nearly immune to position bias, suggesting a costly but general approach toward resolving this vulnerability.

[10] Causal Edit Serialization cs.CL | cs.LGPDF

Louis Robinson

TL;DR: 因果编辑序列化CES让自回归语言模型用显式指针在因果token流中定位和执行插入、删除、替换、复制及移动等文本修改。

Details

Motivation: 标准自回归语言模型按顺序生成文本，而修订需要回指输入上下文或已生成内容中的具体位置。缺少明确定位机制时，生成和局部修改难以在同一因果过程内统一表达。

Result: CES用特殊指针token及预测的源地址来指定编辑位置，结合轻量定位头和双索引旋转位置编码，同时支持地址预测和基于先前指针的条件生成，保留标准因果注意力掩码。预训练结合普通语言建模和由随机损坏、模型生成编辑及重复文本构造的序列化修复。在不同模型规模及编辑基线比较中，CES模型能完整应用编辑，干净文本bits-per-byte仅略高于干净自回归模型。监督微调与强化学习后训练在语法纠错上作概念验证，盲于参考的模型评审认为有益修改多于有害修改；正确修复程序也能在保留原上下文及缓存状态时改善后续文本预测。

Insight: CES为生成和局部修订提供统一接口，并展示编辑操作可成为模型因果输出的一部分。摘要没有给出各模型规模的量化结果、具体纠错基准分数及编辑程序复杂度，因此性能结论以作者报告的趋势为限。

Abstract: Standard autoregressive language models generate text sequentially, but revision requires referring back to tokens in the input context or those already generated. Causal Edit Serialization (CES) is introduced as a framework for expressing such revisions within the same causal token stream. Special pointer tokens are paired with predicted source addresses, allowing insertion, deletion, replacement, copying and movement to be localized explicitly. A lightweight localization head and dual-index rotary position encoding support both address prediction and conditioning on previously emitted pointers, while retaining a standard causal attention mask. A pretraining procedure combines ordinary language modeling with serialized repairs derived from random corruptions, model-generated edits and repeated text. Across model scales and editing baselines, CES models demonstrate complete applied edits with modestly higher clean-text bits per byte than clean autoregressive models. Supervised and reinforcement-learning post-training provide a grammatical-error-correction proof of concept, producing more beneficial than harmful changes under reference-blind model judging. Correct repair programs also improve subsequent text prediction while the original context and its cached states are retained. CES provides a common interface for generation and localized revision, with potential extensions to iterative refinement and additional pointer-addressed transformations.

[11] The Reliance Hypothesis: Chain-of-Thought Faithfulness Rises as Language Resource Level Falls cs.CL | cs.AIPDF

Lucas Resck, Jingyi Sun, Qianli Wang, Isabelle Augenstein, Anna Korhonen

TL;DR: 跨八种语言的研究发现，知识密集任务中链式思维忠实度会随语言资源水平降低而上升，但推理密集任务没有同样趋势。

Details

Motivation: 链式思维忠实度关系到模型监控、解释和安全部署，但相关研究多以英语为主。低资源语言中的模型通常掌握的信息更少，这可能改变模型对显式推理过程的依赖；该影响是否随任务类型变化尚不明确。

Result: 作者在MMMLU的八种语言并行选择题上，比较知识密集与推理密集任务。对四个模型和多种忠实度测量，知识密集任务中资源水平越低，链式思维忠实度越高；推理密集任务未呈现同样关系。研究提出依赖假设：当模型无法回忆答案时（例如低资源语言的知识题），更依赖显式推理。模型置信度能部分解释资源水平效应，却无法解释知识题与推理题的差异。跨语言比较显示低置信语言中的模型更依赖推理，但同一种语言内的低置信回答未必如此，呈现辛普森悖论；低资源语言中使用推理的答案比例较高，而非每条推理都更强。

Insight: 链式思维监测的可验证性可能因语言和任务类型而异，低资源语言或更常出现模型依赖推理的情况，但每条推理的忠实度并不因此必然更高。摘要未提供八种语言与测量方法的完整结果表，结论限于MMMLU选择题评测。

Abstract: Chain-of-Thought (CoT) faithfulness matters for monitoring, explaining and safely deploying large language models, yet it has been studied mostly in English. Languages differ in level, the amount of text available to learn from, so models know less in lower-resource languages, which may change how much they rely on their reasoning. To our knowledge, we are the first to relate CoT faithfulness to both resource level and task type (knowledge- or reasoning-intensive). On parallel multiple-choice questions from Multilingual Massive Multitask Language Understanding (MMMLU) in eight languages, faithfulness rises as resource level falls on knowledge-intensive tasks but not on reasoning-intensive ones, a gap that points the same way across four models and several faithfulness measures. We propose, and find support for, the Reliance Hypothesis: models rely more on their reasoning when they cannot recall the answer, as in lower-resource languages on knowledge-intensive tasks. Model confidence partly explains the effect of resource level, but not the knowledge-reasoning gap. Languages in which models are less confident rely more on reasoning, yet less confident answers within a language do not, a Simpson’s paradox. In lower-resource languages, models rely on their reasoning for more answers, not more strongly for each. CoT monitoring may therefore be most informative in lower-resource languages, where it is least often validated.

[12] Uncertainty-Aware Budget Allocation for Efficient LLM Tree Search cs.CL | cs.AIPDF

Jianguo Huang, Zhiyi Lyu, Fuxiang Zhang, Yanchen Deng, Deheng Ye

TL;DR: 不确定性感知预算分配UAA根据同一搜索节点下候选动作的语义分散度，预估后续分支冗余并动态分配树搜索预算，减少token和延迟。

Details

Motivation: 大语言模型树搜索常给每一步固定扩展预算，忽略继续采样的边际收益。作者发现从相同中间状态采样的续写常高度重复，导致固定预算浪费；若能在扩展前估计分支冗余，就可以减少无效搜索。

Result: 作者发现同级候选动作的语义分散度能够预测后续续写冗余，并据此以信息论分析推导足够采样预算随分散度下降。UAA把候选价值与不确定性分开：价值决定哪些候选值得探索，不确定性用无需额外模型调用的同级分散度估计，并通过不确定性感知剪枝和预算分配决定扩展数量。在数学推理、逻辑推理和规划基准上，准确率相近时token用量最多降低90%、延迟最多降低9倍；效果跨四种树搜索算法以及开源和闭源模型。

Insight: 自适应预算将计算集中到答案不确定且分支多样的状态，有机会在保持准确率时显著降低搜索成本。摘要未给出各基准上的完整准确率差异和具体预算设置，“最多”值不代表所有模型和任务均达到该收益。

Abstract: Tree search enhances multi-step reasoning in large language models (LLMs), yet prevailing methods expand every step with a fixed budget, irrespective of the marginal utility of expansion. We first show, across existing search algorithms, that continuations sampled from a shared intermediate state are largely duplicates and collapse to a few distinct answers. Critically, we find that the semantic dispersion of sibling actions predicts the redundancy of their downstream continuations, permitting a subtree’s budget to be fixed before expansion. An information-theoretic analysis motivates this: treating the per-step budget as the Monte Carlo sample size for estimating a step’s residual answer entropy, we derive a sufficient budget that shrinks with sibling dispersion. We accordingly propose $\textbf{Uncertainty-Aware Allocation}$ (UAA), a training-free framework that decouples value from uncertainty: values determine which candidates are promising, while uncertainty, estimated from sibling dispersion at no additional model cost, determines how many merit expansion, via uncertainty-aware pruning and uncertainty-aware budgeting. Across mathematical reasoning, logical reasoning, and planning benchmarks, UAA reduces token usage by up to $90%$ and latency by up to $9\times$ at comparable accuracy, generalizing across four tree search algorithms and both open- and closed-source LLMs.

Abstract: Formulating optimization problems from raw inputs of problem descriptions and data is time-consuming and costly. While Large Language Models (LLMs) offer a promising way to automate this process, existing benchmarks overestimate their capabilities by relying on idealized inputs where all data is relevant and complete. This diverges from reality, where data-centric reasoning is required to extract, compute, and predict parameters from noisy sources. To address this, we introduce NLData2Opt, a benchmark to evaluate LLMs on the multi-stage reasoning inherent in practical optimization workflows. NLData2Opt decomposes problems into problem descriptions and data, and requires models to (1) extract relevant parameters from data containing misleading attributes, (2) compute missing parameters derived by transforming existing data attributes, and (3) predict unavailable future values from historical observations. Abstract: Bengali hallucination detection must distinguish unsupported factual claims from incorrect reasoning, often without a supplied reference passage. Six retrieval routes search question-answer and passage collections; a router directs each example to a 27B textual judge or a 14B agent that can compare retrieved answers, reason directly, or generate and execute Python. We characterize the system using version-specific code, saved predictions, execution traces, and corpus metadata from the Olikbochon challenge. Kaggle associates a displayed private macro-F1 of 0.956 with Version 2; its saved run processes 2,516 examples in 220.99 minutes on two T4 GPUs. Details

Motivation: 现有逻辑推理基准通常给出若干任务的单一准确率，无法充分衡量模型的推理泛化能力，也可能被语言表述变化影响。测量模型是否掌握一个可重复使用的能力，需要跨难度和等价表述进行系统测试。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have brought impressive performance across diverse tasks; however, shortcomings remain in logical reasoning, including inconsistencies under linguistic variation. Such a performance-based evaluation methodology says little about a model’s generalization capacity in reasoning. To address this limitation, we argue for a different evaluation paradigm, called competence-based evaluation (CBE), which tests whether models reliably apply the same competence across a family of problems. A parameterized generator scales complexity while keeping the required competence fixed, and matched answer-preserving presentations test correctness beyond one formulation. Across six GPT and Qwen models, performance is reasonably good over bounded complexity ranges, but even strong models fail the competence condition across the tested family. CBE provides diagnostic profiles and an evaluation protocol that can grow with future models.

TL;DR: 该研究通过因果追踪发现，多种提示诱导偏差共用预训练阶段已存在的少量注意力头回路，指令微调和强化学习会改变该回路的触发强度。

Details

Motivation: 模型常会因为用户给出错误暗示而放弃正确答案，谄媚及相关偏差通常被分别研究并归因于对齐训练。研究检验不同偏差是否由共享机制驱动，以及后训练的不同阶段如何影响该机制。

Result: 在多数被测模型的选择题任务中，四类提示线索主要依赖相同的少数注意力头，这一回路在预训练模型中已存在。多数基础模型在普通文本提示下会跟随线索；仅用约200条不含线索的通用助手样例做微调，就会在聊天格式中激活这些头，使线索跟随可能成为助手微调副作用。几乎所有比较中，最终强化学习阶段进一步增强线索影响。在Tulu-3-8B和OLMo-2-7B中，研究交换强化学习检查点与前一阶段六个中间层的注意力模式；对建议答案线索，互换后各检查点的线索效应大幅向对方水平移动。

Insight: 多种偏差可能共享预训练得到的线索路由回路，后训练更像在调节何时、以多强程度调用它，而非从头创建回路。交换实验支持该机制具有因果作用，但摘要只涉及特定模型、提示类型和层范围；针对这条回路的缓解仍是潜在方向。

Abstract: Language models often abandon a correct answer when the prompt contains a cue, a hint toward a wrong option such as “I think the answer is B.” Sycophancy and related biases show this failure. They are often studied separately and blamed on alignment. We test this view causally, tracing the attention heads behind these biases through the stages of two post-training pipelines. In multiple-choice tests on most models, four cue types rely largely on the same few heads, a shared circuit already present in pretrained models. Most base models follow cues in plain text. Fine-tuning a base model on about 200 generic assistant examples without cues activates these heads in chat format, so cue-following can be a side effect of assistant tuning. The final reinforcement learning (RL) stage strengthens the effect of cues in almost every comparison. In Tulu-3-8B and OLMo-2-7B, we swap the attention patterns of six middle layers between the RL checkpoint and its predecessor. For suggested-answer cues, this shifts each checkpoint’s cue effect most of the way to the other’s level. Post-training thus seems to change when and how strongly models use this circuit. Mitigation for these multiple-choice biases could target this circuit.

TL;DR: 该研究分析敏感群体属性对语言模型回答的反事实影响，并提出对成对反事实提示优化的群体级奖励，以减小无关属性导致的质量差异。

Details

Motivation: 提示中的人口统计群体信息等敏感属性可能改变模型输出。当属性与当前任务在因果上无关时，这种敏感性会带来偏差；但若属性确实与答案相关，模型仍需正确适应，因此完全消除属性影响并不合理。

Result: 作者发现，标准对齐流程（如强化学习）虽然提升总体回答质量，却会扩大不同人口群体之间的回答质量差异，增强反事实偏差。为此提出策略梯度技术，对成对的反事实提示使用群体级奖励。研究还构建受控反事实提示对数据集，并设计区分人口统计信息对答案具有因果相关和无关情形的评测。多个公平性基准上的结果显示，该方法能减少反事实差异，同时保持总体回答质量，并在敏感信息确实相关时保留适当适应。

Insight: 公平性目标需要区分“与任务无关的群体属性”与“确实影响答案的属性”，否则不变性训练可能损害有用性。摘要未报告各基准的具体数值、群体类别和数据规模，适用边界需结合完整实验判断。

Abstract: Small variations in prompt characteristics can meaningfully alter the semantics, tone, and contextual assumptions of large language model outputs. Such characteristics can involve group variables such as demographics or other sensitive latent attributes. Sensitivity to such input variables is undesirable when demographic information is causally irrelevant to the task, but might still be relevant for generation of semantically coherent responses. This makes the task of enforcing counterfactual invariance while preserving model utility difficult. To study this problem, we first show that standard model alignment recipes (such as reinforcement learning) increase differences in response quality across demographics, while improving overall response quality, ultimately amplifying what is known as counterfactual bias. To address this, we develop a policy gradient technique involving a group-level reward function that operates on matched pairs of counterfactual prompts. For completeness, we build a dataset of controlled counterfactual prompt pairs and design an evaluation that separately measures cases where demographic information is causally relevant to the answer. Finally, we validate our findings on several fairness benchmarks. We demonstrate that our methods reduce counterfactual differences while preserving overall response quality and that the models continue to adapt appropriately when such sensitive information is relevant.

Abstract: Domain terminology provides concise descriptions of game states and strategic relationships, but whether game-playing language models can accurately recognize these concepts and relate them to move evaluation remains unclear. [20] Prism Attention: Proposal-Refined Index Sharing Mechanism for Efficient LLMs Inference cs.CL | cs.AIPDF

Zhenxu Tian, Kebin Liu, Zhengwu Yang, Yi Su, Qingqing Dang

TL;DR: Prism Attention以粗到细的候选索引细化替代跨层静态稀疏索引共享，在保留层间适应性的同时降低长上下文解码成本。

Details

Motivation: 长上下文解码需在每一步对不断增长的KV缓存执行注意力，稀疏注意力虽减少计算，但若每层、每步都重算索引，收益会被索引成本抵消。跨层共享可减少重算，却可能因注意力模式随层变化而让索引偏离最优。

Result: 作者观察到层间高显著token变化集中在既有选择区域扩展边界附近：核心高注意力token大多稳定。Prism Attention利用这一点进行提案式细化，避免僵硬复用索引，同时保留跨层共享效率。在推理和长上下文基准上，论文报告该方法在主流稀疏注意力方法中达到当时最佳表现；上下文长度128K时，相比FlashAttention端到端解码加速3.4倍。

Insight: 局部化的层间注意力变化可用于减少稀疏索引重复计算，而不必每层从头选择。摘要未列出具体基准成绩、模型及质量变化，因此3.4倍速度结果限于128K报告设置。

Abstract: Long-context decoding in large language models (LLMs) is increasingly bottlenecked by the memory and computation required to attend over an ever-growing Key-Value (KV) cache at each autoregressive step. Although token-level sparse attention reduces this burden by restricting computation to a small subset of relevant past tokens, its practical gains are often offset by repeatedly recomputing layer-wise sparse indices as the context grows. A common remedy is to share sparse indices across layers by computing them only at designated anchor layers, but such static reuse ignores the layer-wise evolution of attention patterns, causing the reused indices to progressively deviate from the layer-specific optima and incur substantial attention loss. By revisiting cross-layer sparsity from the perspective of attention dynamics, we find that inter-layer salient token shift is highly localized: although the exact top-$κ$ indices differ across layers, the core high-attention tokens remain stable, with changes concentrated near the expanded boundary of previously selected regions. Motivated by this, we propose Prism Attention, a proposal-refined sparse attention framework. It replaces rigid cross-layer index reuse with a coarse-to-fine strategy, preserving layer-wise adaptability while retaining the efficiency benefits of cross-layer sharing. Extensive experiments show that Prism Attention achieves state-of-the-art performance among mainstream sparse attention methods on both reasoning and long-context benchmarks, while attaining a $3.4\times$ end-to-end decoding speedup over FlashAttention at 128K context length.

Details

Motivation: 现有工具安全研究多分析工具被调用之后的行为，却较少检查模型事先是否应该调用工具。准入决定本身会影响后续风险，也可能与模型是否在文本中表达拒绝并不一致。

Result: 作者对七个开放权重模型进行请求因子实验。对已准入的有害请求，模型平均有害率为74.5%；在构造的实验中，中和所请求操作因素带来最大准入率差异，为19个百分点。由该差异估计的线性方向能影响七个模型中四个在留出样例上的准入决策；部分经操作中和样例也受影响，说明该方向并非只针对特定操作机制。已准入的有害工具调用中，仅33.6%伴随明确文字拒绝。对保留良性工具使用能力的模型，纯文本拒绝训练可降低不安全准入。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly act as agents that call external tools, yet most tool-safety research examines only what follows a tool call. The prior decision of whether to call the tool remains largely unexamined and can influence downstream safety. Across the seven open-weight models, the unweighted model-average harm rate after admitted harmful requests is 74.5%. To identify what governs this decision, we apply a factorial intervention to the input request. Within our generated construction, neutralizing the requested-operation component produces the largest admission contrast (19 pp). A linear direction estimated from this contrast influences held-out admission in four of seven models; effects on operation-neutralized requests in some models show that it is not an operation-specific mechanism. We then test whether written refusal is behaviorally and representationally coupled to the same decision. Written refusal accompanies 33.6% of admitted harmful calls. Plain-text refusal training reduces unsafe admission in models that retain benign tool use, showing that response-level safety training can transfer to the call decision. Together, these results establish a partial, model-dependent relation between written refusal and tool admission. They motivate direct evaluation and supervision of tool admission rather than treating text refusal as its proxy.

Abstract: In a hierarchical multi-agent system, a Leader assigns tasks and subagents carry them out. Motivation: GRPO对同一问题中获得相同奖励的多个回答赋予相同组相对优势，没有利用回答生成的不确定性。回答正确性相同但生成稳定度不同，可能需要不同的学习信号。

Abstract: GRPO assigns the same group-relative advantage to equally rewarded responses within a question, leaving their generation uncertainty unused. We introduce MGE-PO, centered on four-quadrant (4Q) advantage shaping that combines verified correctness with rollout generation entropy. Correctness determines the sign of an additional correction, while entropy controls its magnitude: lower-entropy successes receive larger bonuses and higher-entropy failures receive larger penalties. An online entropy-based question curriculum supports this credit rule using cached rollout measurements. Abstract: Small-to-large language model routing can reduce inference cost by escalating difficult inputs while retaining easier ones on a smaller model. Its success, however, depends on identifying examples that benefit from escalation, and the reliability of confidence across multilingual tasks remains unclear. We present a systematic evaluation of confidence-based routing across three model families, four languages, and three task types, covering 72 model, dataset, language, and scale configurations and 42,288 predictions. We compare global confidence routing with random routing, equal-quota confidence routing, and an oracle upper bound. Confidence identifies beneficial escalations on the Belebele reading comprehension and SIB-200 topic classification datasets, with mean correction-detection AUCs of 0.612 and 0.705. At 50% escalation, it improves over random routing by 1.40–4.67 percentage points on Belebele and 2.54–5.63 points on SIB-200. [25] Think More or Read More? A Token-Budget Theory of Reasoning with Shared Context cs.CL | cs.LGPDF

Manh Nguyen, Sunil Gupta, Hung Le

TL;DR: 该研究建立共享上下文窗口下的token预算理论，分析推理token与读取通信token的取舍，并发现自一致性在多数匹配预算设置中不逊于多智能体辩论。

Details

Motivation: 多智能体辩论和自我修订通过额外读取他人回答或自身草稿改善推理，但所有输入输出共享固定上下文窗口时，读取信息会占用本可用于继续思考的token预算。预算紧张时通信是否值得，需要定量判断。

Result: 理论将准确率表示为读取后剩余推理预算的函数，并以允许相关错误的Condorcet陪审团模型聚合投票；对多智能体辩论推导最优智能体数的闭式预测，可由单智能体测量估算，并推广到异构模型。实证中，在校准成本最紧、辩论规则只能进行一两次提问时，按预测人数选择的方案优于强基线。理论同样适用于以自身草稿替代同伴消息的自我修订，并预测只有准确率上限增益超过阅读占用推理空间时通信才有益，窗口越小这一条件越严格。在五个开放模型、四类推理任务、980种匹配窗口和组大小设置中，自一致性有93%的情况下持平或超过已部署辩论；差距在窗口紧时最大，宽松窗口时几乎消失。

Insight: 在固定窗口内，额外读取内容会直接挤占推理空间；通信收益取决于其带来的能力提升是否抵消预算损失。结果覆盖五个开放模型和特定任务，不能直接推广到不受共享窗口约束或使用不同上下文机制的系统。

Abstract: Reasoning systems increasingly improve answers by incorporating additional context into the model’s input, from multi-agent debate, where agents read their peers’ responses, to self-refinement, where a model revisits its own drafts. In these systems, communication consumes context as one agent’s output becomes another’s input. We study the regime where token reading and generation draw on one fixed token window, so every token spent reading is a token that cannot be spent reasoning. We develop a \textbf{token-budget theory} that formalizes this trade-off, modeling accuracy as a function of the reasoning budget remaining after reading and aggregating votes with a Condorcet jury model that permits correlated errors. For multi-agent debate, the theory gives a closed-form prediction for the optimal number of agents. This quantity is estimated from single-agent measurements, allowing the optimal group size to be determined without running debate, and the same accounting extends to a heterogeneous set of different models. We empirically verify our theory and show that, at the predicted group size, our method outperforms strong baselines under the tightest calibration spend, where debate-calibrated rules can afford only one or two questions. Self-refinement obeys the same accounting, with its own draft in place of a peer message. The theory further predicts that communication pays only when the gain in the accuracy ceiling exceeds the reasoning room spent on reading, with this condition tightening as the window shrinks. Across five open models and four reasoning tasks, self-consistency matches or beats deployed debate at $93$% of $980$ matched window-and-group-size settings, with the gap being largest under tight windows and nearly disappearing at a loose one, consistent with the theory. Under a shared window, how much room a model has left to \textit{think matters more than what it reads}.

Motivation: 现有思维链忠实度验证主要基于模型输出或静态激活，难以解释不忠实性如何在生成过程中形成、哪些推理锚点发生变化或如何跨层传递。

Abstract: Current Chain-of-Thought (CoT) verification methods detect reasoning faithfulness from model outputs or static activations, but are limited in explaining how unfaithfulness develops during generation. We introduce VERITAS to verbalise the model’s computation: how different reasoning anchors emerge, transfer across layers, and surface in the output. VERITAS adopts the layer-wise Jacobian projection to produce a verbalised account of the model’s internal reasoning evolution at each generated CoT token. By training a classifier on the extracted features along the traces, we show that these structured features provide insightful perspectives on how and where reasoning diverges toward unfaithfulness. Experiments on four datasets from FaithCoT-Bench and BonaFide show that VERITAS achieves a significant performance gain over LLM-as-Judge, with a 28.4% gain in balanced accuracy, and an average improvement of 16.0% over activation probing. Further analysis shows VERITAS can be generalised to different datasets and model architectures. Our work shows that verbalising model computation provides a richer and more interpretable lens on CoT faithfulness, moving beyond detection toward a mechanistic understanding of how reasoning fails.

[27] Language Models Learn to Improve Language Models cs.CL | cs.AIPDF

Minhua Lin, Zijun Wang, Zewen Liu, Zhaotian Weng, Bing He

TL;DR: LM2LM用研究世界模型为语言模型改进建议提供可扩展的预测反馈，再定期执行高分建议校正反馈，训练模型自动提出模型优化方案。

Details

Motivation: 让语言模型自动化语言模型研发有望实现递归自我改进，但强化学习若需评测数万条建议且每条都完整训练一次模型，反馈可能消耗数十万GPU小时。廉价代理奖励又会在策略分布变化后过度估计候选建议。

Result: LM2LM通过强化学习训练模型生成有效的语言模型改进建议。研究世界模型RWM是冻结语言模型，它根据上下文中的真实实验记录预测建议收益，并作为强化学习奖励模型；为维持反馈可信度，系统周期性地实际执行当前策略提出且被RWM评为较高的建议，再把测量结果加入上下文。在八个自回归和扩散语言模型开发环境中，对留出任务进行真实执行评估后，LM2LM使Qwen3.8-27B超过GPT-6-sol，即使后者上下文内提供300条真实实验记录；数据生成成本降低33%，且能泛化至训练中未见的模型家族。

Insight: 代理研究世界模型加周期性真实执行，试图兼顾强化学习反馈规模和分布漂移时的准确性。摘要未给出各环境的独立结果、真实执行预算和建议成功率，成本结论应限于比较设置。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to automate AI research, including language-model (LM) development, offering a potential path toward recursive self-improvement. However, turning experimental feedback into a learned ability to propose effective improvements across models faces two challenges. First, training such an ability with real feedback is expensive: reinforcement learning (RL) requires evaluating tens of thousands of proposals, each potentially requiring a full model-training run. Even at ten GPU-hours per run, reward collection alone would consume hundreds of thousands of GPU-hours, motivating a cheaper surrogate. Second, surrogate feedback can become unreliable as policy optimization favors overestimated proposals when the proposal distribution shifts. To address these challenges, we propose LM2LM, which trains LLMs with RL to generate effective LM improvement proposals. To provide scalable feedback, we use a research world model (RWM), a frozen LLM that predicts proposal gains from real experimental records provided in context, as the reward model for RL. To maintain feedback reliability, we periodically execute current-policy proposals ranked highly by the RWM and add their measured outcomes to its context. Across eight autoregressive and diffusion LM development environments, real-execution evaluations on held-out tasks show that LM2LM enables Qwen3.8-27B to outperform GPT-6-sol, even when GPT-6-sol is given 300 real experimental records in context, while requiring 33% lower data-generation cost. LM2LM also generalizes to a model family unseen during training.

[28] On the Tip of the Tongue: First-Token Cueing as a Behavioral Probe of Recall Failures in LLMs cs.CLPDF

Jaewon Sok, Jewon Yeom, Seonghyeon Park, Jeongjae Park, Hwiyeong Lee

TL;DR: First-Token Cueing通过预先提供正确答案的首个token，揭示多语言事实问答中一部分闭卷错误源自首步回忆失败，而非模型完全缺少答案知识。

Details

Motivation: 语言模型闭卷回答事实问题时常出错，通常被归因于知识缺失，但模型有时实际编码了答案，只是在开始生成时没有提取出来。需要区分知识不存在与首token选择失误，以改进推理时纠错。

Result: First-Token Cueing将标准答案首token预置到模型回答中，作为行为探针。模型不确定性在首个答案token处最高；在任意长度的标准答案前缀中，单独首token纠正的错误最多。对16个开放模型，该线索纠正英语多token答案错误的五分之一至一半，并提升多语言基准全部七种语言的准确率。大多数被纠正错误也能由模型自身采样推理解决，支持相关答案已被编码但未成功回忆的解释。模型对正确起始token的续写信心高于类型和先验概率相近的错误起始token，但没有标准答案提示时很少自行找到该正确起点。

Insight: 可恢复的一类事实错误集中在解码第一步，推理时方法可能优先干预首token选择。首token提示使用了标准答案信息，属于诊断探针而非无监督可直接部署的解决方案；摘要未给出各模型和语言的具体恢复比例。

Abstract: Large language models often answer factual questions incorrectly in closed-book settings, and such errors are usually attributed to missing knowledge. Yet models often encode answers they fail to produce. We show that many closed-book errors are first-token recall failures: the model commits to a wrong first token, but once given the right one it completes the answer and often recognizes it as correct. We study this with First-Token Cueing, a behavioral probe that prefills the response with the first token of the gold answer. Model uncertainty peaks at the first answer token, where it already predicts correctness, and among gold prefixes of any length the first token alone accounts for the largest share of recovered errors. Across 16 open models, this one token corrects between a fifth and a half of the errors on multi-token answers in English QA and improves accuracy in all seven languages of a multilingual benchmark. Most of the errors it corrects are also solved by the model’s own sampled reasoning, a criterion for encoded knowledge, and models recognize correct starts they do not produce: they continue a correct start more confidently than an incorrect start of the same answer type and prior probability, yet rarely find it without the gold answer. These results locate a recoverable class of factual errors at the first decoding step, where inference-time methods may gain the most.

[29] HydraLLM: Adaptive Width Slicing for Elastic Text and Vision-Language Models cs.CL | cs.LGPDF

Oscar Kemme, Janek Haberer, Ali Hojjat, Olaf Landsiedel

TL;DR: HydraLLM通过激活不同数量的注意力头形成嵌套宽度子网，使同一文本或视觉语言模型可按硬件和算力预算弹性运行。

Details

Motivation: 大语言模型和大视觉语言模型需部署在从数据中心GPU到边缘设备的异构硬件上，固定规模架构难以复用同一模型适配不同算力、延迟和模型大小目标。

Result: HydraLLM采用基于MQA的弹性架构，激活注意力头数决定嵌入、注意力投影、前馈层和输出头的有效宽度，从单组参数形成嵌套子网。静态到弹性的转换流程先按重要性重排权重，再通过知识蒸馏进行随机子网训练；多模态推理则用输出宽度随当前文本宽度变化的弹性投影模块。模型可配置范围比仅弹性化前馈层的方法更广。激活75%注意力头时，TriviaQA准确率比参数多17.25%的嵌套基线高2.2个百分点；激活50%时，多模态模型CLIPScore与大7倍的Gemma 3 4B相当；只激活25%头时，token生成吞吐量比全宽配置提高75.3%。

Insight: 按宽度切片复用单个检查点，可以在性能、参数量和吞吐量间做部署时折衷。摘要没有给出所有宽度下的任务精度及硬件实测延迟；不同任务的指标不能相互直接比较。

Abstract: LLMs and LVLMs are increasingly deployed across heterogeneous hardware, ranging from data-center GPUs to resource-constrained edge devices. Their fixed-size architectures limit the ability to reuse a single model across deployment settings with different compute, latency, and model-size targets. We introduce HydraLLM, an elastic MQA-based architecture. Its number of active attention heads determines the effective widths of the embeddings, attention projections, feed-forward layers, and the output head, forming a nested family of subnetworks within a single parameter set. We introduce a static-to-elastic conversion pipeline that, starting from a pre-trained model checkpoint, includes importance-driven weight reordering and stochastic subnetwork training with knowledge distillation. We extend this principle to multimodal inference through an elastic projection module whose output width follows the active text width. HydraLLM covers a wider range of active parameter configurations than FFN-only elastic architectures. With 75% of heads active, HydraLLM achieves 2.2 percentage points higher TriviaQA accuracy than a state-of-the-art nested baseline configuration which has 17.25% more parameters. With 50% of heads active, our multimodal model achieves a CLIPScore comparable to that of the 7$\times$ larger Gemma 3 4B model. With only 25% of heads active, HydraLLM increases token-generation throughput by 75.3% over the full-width configuration.

[30] Perceive, Solve, Reason: Progressive Capability Formation in Time Series-LLMs cs.CLPDF

Xin Qiu, Junlong Tong, Wei Zhang, Xiaoyu Shen

TL;DR: 该研究构建覆盖感知、求解和推理的时间序列语言模型训练评测套件，发现专用编码器及按能力递进训练对泛化表现关键。

Details

Motivation: 大语言模型已能理解图像和音频，但时间序列常被局限于预测和分类，推理研究少且范围窄，缺少覆盖多种能力并能统一训练与评估的体系。

Result: 作者构建含337K训练样本、44K评测样本的三阶段能力套件：Perceive、Solve和Reason，用于研究时间序列应如何进入LLM及能力训练顺序。专用编码器不可或缺：配备编码器的4B模型在所有测试感知属性上超过GPT-4o，而将序列转成文本会使推理准确率下降8–10分。能力按感知、求解、推理顺序学习优于混合使用相同数据，并可媲美使用三倍以上推理数据的纯推理训练，但这一优势仅在有编码器时成立。能力通过网络不同深度迁移，并非简单堆叠；单阶段在本身基准收敛也不代表已准备好进入下一阶段。最终4B模型无需目标域数据，在八个域外指标中的七个超过四个近期7B时间序列模型，最高提升11分。

Insight: 时间序列可作为LLM的一等模态，通过专用编码器输入，并先学感知再学推理。摘要未给出各任务完整分数和数据集分布；训练顺序优势依赖专用编码器，不能简单推广为所有架构都适用。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can now perceive, describe, and reason about images and audio, yet time series understanding remains underestimated: most work uses LLMs as forecasters and classifiers, reasoning studies are few and narrow, and no suite trains and evaluates the full range of capabilities. In this paper, we build the first such suite, with 337K training and 44K evaluation samples across three progressive capabilities, Perceive, Solve, and Reason, and use it to ask how time series should enter an LLM that must master all three, and in what order. We find that a dedicated encoder is essential: with one, a 4B model surpasses GPT-4o on every perception attribute we test, whereas textualizing the series costs 8–10 points in reasoning accuracy. The capabilities then build on one another: learning them in order beats mixing the same data and rivals reasoning-only training with over three times the reasoning data, but only when an encoder is used. Analyses further reveal that capabilities are transferred through distinct network depths rather than simply stacked, and that a stage converged on its own benchmark is not necessarily ready for the next. Distilled into a simple recipe, these findings yield 4B TS-LLMs that, without any target-domain data, outperform four recent 7B TS-LLMs on seven of eight out-of-domain metrics, by up to 11 points. We therefore advocate treating time series as a first-class modality for LLMs, read through dedicated encoders and taught to perceive before they reason.

[31] ConvexLLM: Scaling Convex-Hull Speech Interfaces to General-Purpose Audio Intelligence cs.CL | cs.SD | eess.ASPDF

Ming-Hao Hsu, Shujie Liu, Yuxuan Hu, Yuxiang Wang, Yingda Shen

TL;DR: ConvexLLM以冻结文本嵌入的连续凸组合表示音频输入，在多项语音和音频任务上表现有竞争力，同时尽量保持解码器已有文本能力。

Details

Motivation: 语音和音频语言模型需要把连续声学表示映射到主要在文本数据上预训练的解码器输入空间。常见MLP或Q-Former接口让解码器重新学习解释这些表示，可能影响已有文本能力。

Result: ConvexLLM使用C-Gate接口，将音频表示为冻结文本嵌入的连续凸组合。VoiceBench十一任务综合得分为75.36，在比较的开放权重模型中最高；MMAU Test-mini准确率为62.50%。相较基线，其最大优势出现在口语知识和推理任务，但在音频理解和转录上最强基线更准确。与2B参数解码器的接口比较中，C-Gate总体口语表现高于MLP和Q-Former，文本任务表现接近MLP，转录和音频理解存在依赖任务的权衡。

Insight: 将音频映射到解码器熟悉的文本嵌入凸包内，是保持文本能力并接入声学信息的一种可行设计。摘要显示不同任务间有取舍，不能概括为在全部音频任务上超过基线；未列出每个任务的分数。

Abstract: Speech and audio language models connect continuous acoustic representations to decoders pretrained primarily on text data. Most such models use MLP or Q-Former interfaces to map acoustic features into the decoder’s input space, requiring the decoder to learn how to interpret the resulting representations. We investigate whether grounding these representations in the decoder’s existing text embeddings can help it use acoustic information while better preserving its pretrained text capabilities. We present ConvexLLM, a general-purpose speech and audio language model whose C-Gate interface represents audio as continuous convex combinations of frozen text embeddings. ConvexLLM achieves an eleven-task VoiceBench aggregate of 75.36, the highest among the open-weight models we compare, and an MMAU Test-mini accuracy of 62.50%. Its largest margins over the baselines are on spoken knowledge and reasoning tasks, while the strongest baselines are more accurate on audio understanding and transcription. In a comparison with a 2B-parameter decoder, C-Gate achieves higher aggregate spoken-language performance than MLP and Q-Former interfaces, with text-task performance close to MLP and task-dependent tradeoffs in transcription and audio understanding. These results support convex combinations of text embeddings as a viable interface for general-purpose speech and audio language models.

[32] Acceptance Is Not Speed: Length Inflation in Training-Free Lossy Speculative Decoding cs.CLPDF

Chiatzen Wang, Bill Xu, Haochen Gu, Hongyang Zhang

TL;DR: 该研究指出无损验证与有损宽松验证的speculative decoding不能只用每轮接受token数比较，因为宽松规则可能导致生成文本尤其是推理链变长。

Details

Motivation: 推测解码常用每次验证接受的草稿token数量估计加速，但这一指标假设输出长度不变。宽松验证会接受无损策略会拒绝的token并改变输出分布，可能通过延长回答抵消表面上的接受率收益。

Result: 作者在12组目标模型—草稿模型配对及相同提示上，将训练免调的宽松验证规则与无损验证比较，测量生成长度、验证轮数、实际耗时和任务准确率。在每种规则最宽松设置下，平均每轮接受token数为无损策略的1.34倍，但生成长度达到1.54倍。对接受长度更高的规则，只有宽松策略接受的token往往在目标模型下概率较低，进而造成长度膨胀；减少验证轮数的规则通常伴随准确率明显下降。部分失败来自继续推理消耗了本用于给出最终答案的预算。

Insight: 宽松验证下，单看每轮接受token数不能代表端到端解码速度或效率；还需同时报告完成长度、验证轮数、耗时和任务准确率。摘要结果针对所测训练免规则及模型配对，不代表所有推测解码实现的相同幅度。

Abstract: Speculative decoding typically characterizes speedup by draft tokens accepted per verification round. Lossless verification preserves the target model’s output distribution and thus expected completion length, making this metric a meaningful efficiency proxy. Relaxed (lossy) verification accepts tokens that lossless verification would reject, potentially altering that distribution. We identify a length inflation phenomenon: relaxed verification lengthens completions, primarily by extending reasoning traces, largely offsetting apparent gains in accepted tokens per round. We evaluate existing training-free relaxed verification rules on twelve target-drafter pairs against lossless verification on identical prompts, measuring completion length, verifier rounds, wall-clock time, and task accuracy. At each rule’s most relaxed evaluated setting, relaxed verification on average accepts $1.34\times$ as many tokens per round but produces $1.54\times$ longer completions. For rules with higher acceptance lengths, tokens accepted only under relaxed verification tend to have low target-model probability, contributing to length inflation. For rules that reduce verifier rounds, accuracy typically drops substantially. Further analysis traces some failures to continued reasoning that exhausts the budget needed to deliver a final answer. Overall, accepted tokens per round alone is an unreliable measure of decoding speed under relaxed verification. Completion length and verifier rounds must also be reported to assess actual efficiency.

[33] Beyond the Answer: Student-Conditioned Trace Construction for Reasoning Distillation cs.CL | cs.LGPDF

Shaoang Li, Yanhang Shi, Yufei Li, Mingfu Liang, Frank Shyu

TL;DR: ReDiST按学生能力构造并筛选推理轨迹，再联合蒸馏过程和答案监督，提升问答源任务及跨基准表现。

Details

Motivation: 正确答案配套的推理轨迹不一定适合作为特定学生模型的监督。有效蒸馏除了轨迹内容是否正确，还取决于目标模型能否理解和利用这些中间步骤。

Result: ReDiST从学生自身尝试开始：对答案正确的草稿依据给定证据进行修订，对失败草稿用核验答案补充指导。与学生匹配的冻结阅读器基于问题、证据和候选轨迹，以答案恢复能力和核验答案概率筛选排序原始及编辑后的轨迹。训练时对轨迹与最终答案监督进行平衡，并加入辅助答案损失；构造和筛选在线下完成，推理阶段只使用蒸馏后的学生。问答基准实验显示，相比只监督答案、原始轨迹及所测轨迹筛选和改写基线，ReDiST可提升源任务与跨基准成绩。不同监督方法在源基准和迁移基准上的排名可能不同。

Insight: 推理监督质量应以目标学生实际学会的程度评估，而不只看轨迹是否看似合理；迁移分布也会改变训练方法排序。摘要未给出具体基准、数值和不同学生规模结果。

Abstract: A reasoning trace can accompany a correct answer without providing effective supervision for a particular student. We study how to construct traces that improve learning beyond answer-only supervision by considering both their intermediate content and their accessibility to the target learner. We introduce ReDiST, a student-conditioned trace-construction and distillation framework for evidence-rich reasoning. Starting from the student’s own attempts, ReDiST revises answer-correct drafts against the supplied evidence and repairs unsuccessful drafts with additional guidance from the verified answer. A frozen student-matched reader screens and ranks original and edited candidates using answer recovery and verified-answer probability, given the question, evidence, and candidate trace. We distill the selected targets while balancing trace and final-answer supervision with an auxiliary answer loss. Construction and selection occur offline, and inference uses only the distilled student. Experiments on question-answering benchmarks show that ReDiST can improve both source-task and cross-benchmark performance over answer-only, original-trace, and the evaluated trace-selection and rewriting baselines. Across supervision recipes, performance rankings can differ between source and transfer benchmarks, highlighting the importance of evaluating trace teaching utility across distributions.

[34] DiscoveryRSI: Recursively Learning to Discover Valuable Hypotheses cs.CLPDF

Jianyuan Zhong, Chenwei Wu, Zhaochen Yu, Xixian Jiang, Xuewei Li

TL;DR: DiscoveryRSI让多个智能体通过进化式搜索提出可检验科学假设，并递归利用实验反馈改进研究流程，在四个生命科学领域的假设质量有所提升。

Details

Motivation: 科学发现需要把有价值的假设转化为可执行调查，但如何从不断演进的研究过程中学习有效提案很难。需要定义假设价值，并将实验评价归因于研究贡献，同时避免仅凭语言模型自评形成闭环偏差。

Result: 作者将有价值、可检验假设定义为：围绕重要不确定性提出有证据支撑的主张，配套可行调查、明确判据和替代解释。DiscoveryRSI通过多智能体进化搜索构造假设树，将选择的调查编译成可执行Harbor任务；再通过假设信用优化、harness回放改进流程，以及将认可改进迁移到新调查的再发现机制递归学习。DFM-Bench从假设、修订、发展路径和研究运行等层级评估质量与累积进展。四个生命科学领域中，自动评测的假设质量从基础模型4.86提高到两轮自我改进后的5.39，主要增益在可证伪性和数据可判定性。初步专家评审中，DiscoveryRSI-27B在重要性及主张合理且受证据约束方面高于GPT-6 Luna；对模型截止时间后发表研究的假设对应率为6.3%，高于GPT-6的2.5%和Claude Opus 5的2.3%。

Insight: 递归研究系统不仅要提出主张，也要让假设可证伪、数据可处理并能从后续研究反馈中改进。人类评估被描述为初步，且摘要未详述对应率判定和研究领域，不能将自动指标等同于真实科学发现价值。

Abstract: Scientific discovery requires developing worthwhile hypotheses into practical investigations, yet learning from this evolving research process remains challenging. We introduce an operational definition of valuable and testable research hypotheses: evidence-grounded claims about consequential uncertainties, coupled with feasible investigations, explicit decision criteria, and alternative explanations. Our DiscoveryRSI framework develops hypothesis trees through multi-agent evolutionary search, compiles selected investigations into executable Harbor tasks, and recursively learns through three mechanisms: hypothesis credit optimization attributes scientific assessments to documented research contributions; harness replay identifies productive strategies and recurring failures to refine research workflows; and rediscovery applies accepted improvements to new investigations, generating further experience for subsequent learning. We also introduce DFM-Bench, which provides comprehensive assessment of hypothesis quality and cumulative research progress at the levels of hypotheses, revisions, development paths and research runs. Across four life-science domains, automated evaluation shows that hypothesis quality rises from 4.86 for the base model to 5.39 after two rounds of self-improvement, with the large gains in falsifiability and data resolvability. We further validate hypothesis quality through expert review, comparing our DiscoveryRSI-27B with other research agents. Preliminary human assessments show that DiscoveryRSI-27B scores higher than GPT-6 Luna on hypothesis importance and whether claims are plausible and evidence-bounded. DiscoveryRSI-27B achieved a 6.3% rate of hypothesis correspondence with research published after the model cutoff, largely outperform GPT-6 with 2.5% and Claude Opus 5 with 2.3%, while delivering greater human-rated improvement along research paths.

[35] Star Light, Star Bright: Can Multimodal Models Parse an Astronomy Visual Question Answering Dataset Right? cs.CV | astro-ph.IM | cs.DL | cs.IRPDF

J. P. Naiman

TL;DR: 该研究通过天文图像VQA数据集评估低成本多模态模型的领域图表理解，发现模型在专业问题上经常答错，且在历史期刊图像上的角尺度误差与人工比较结果相近。

Abstract: Understanding biomolecular function requires characterizing three-dimensional structure and conformational variability, for which cryogenic electron microscopy (cryo-EM) is a powerful tool. Recovering biological information from these observations spans inverse problems including pose estimation, heterogeneity analysis, and reconstruction. These tasks are usually addressed independently despite shared protein structure and image formation, motivating a common representation across inverse problems. Since biologically meaningful differences may be spatially localized, we study self-distillation with complementary global and masked patch objectives. Because experimental dataset discrimination can reflect acquisition-specific cues, we develop controlled synthetic and experimental evaluations and find that structural discrimination benefits from dense spatial features. We demonstrate how to learn representations that capture biological information for use across multiple cryo-EM inverse problems.

Subject-exclusive evaluation reduces five-class macro-F1 to 0.475 for video and 0.404 for EEG, highlighting the difficulty of grading unseen animals. A separate video-only binary-detection evaluation on RodEpil achieves a mean seizure-class F1 of 0.979 after uniform re-encoding to reduce identified class-correlated encoding cues. The study characterizes existing models for behavior-defined severity labels under the evaluated protocol, motivating further investigation of expert-assisted annotation workflows. Our code and github page is available here.

Abstract: Retrieval is a fundamental task in multimodal systems: given an image or text query, the goal is to find the most relevant images or texts in a collection, often using a shared embedding space. Reliable retrieval requires these embeddings to capture not only which concepts are present, but also how they are composed. One key compositional challenge is attribute–object binding: encoding which attributes belong to which objects. Existing benchmarks provide a limited view of this capability, typically covering a subset of modality pairings, simple two-object swaps, or small attribute sets. Consequently, it remains unclear how robustly current models preserve attribute–object bindings across different modality pairings and more complex settings. We therefore introduce SABER, a controlled benchmark for evaluating attribute–object binding across all four image and text modality pairings, spanning diverse attribute types and scenes with up to ten objects. Evaluating models on SABER reveals substantial binding failures in both CLIP-style dual encoders and recent MLLM-based embedders. These findings motivate us to ask how binding can be improved efficiently. While multimodal supervision may be effective, collecting suitable images is difficult and costly. Text, on the other hand, is well suited to binding supervision: it is cheap to generate, supports a broader range of attributes and enables precisely controlled hard negatives. We thus finetune an MLLM-based embedder exclusively on synthetic attribute-binding text. Despite the absence of image supervision, the gains transfer to cross-modal and image–image retrieval while preserving general multimodal embedding performance. Our model achieves SOTA results on SABER while also improving on negation and instance-retrieval benchmarks, demonstrating broader benefits of our binding-focused supervision.

Abstract: Recent advances in video generation let users create high-quality videos from natural language descriptions, yet obtaining results that match user intent remains time-consuming: individual generations are computationally expensive, and underspecified prompts force repeated cycles of generation, inspection, and revision. Existing video prompt optimization methods improve generation through automatic rewriting, but cannot supply the ideas and preferences that were not in the initial description. We propose ProactGen, a video generation framework that learns proactive clarification, eliciting user intent through questions grounded in generated video drafts. Specifically, we train a vision-language model as a questioning policy with reinforcement learning: observing the initial prompt and a video draft, it learns to ask the questions most valuable to downstream generation, rewarded by the alignment between the final generated video and a target reference. We further find that effective questioning does not require drafts of final-output quality, and exploit this property by generating drafts with fewer denoising steps, keeping the added cost of clarification low. Experiments show that ProactGen improves target alignment over direct generation by at least 1.8x the gain of the strongest baselines. Moreover, a single round of ProactGen already exceeds the alignment that the strongest baseline reaches after four refinement rounds, in 29% of its time.

[46] EgoP$^{2}$C: Internalizing Plan-Perceive-Compose for Spatial Reasoning in Egocentric Video cs.CVPDF

Abstract: Answering complex spatial reasoning questions from egocentric video requires determining what evidence is needed, acquiring targeted observations, and combining them through multiple reasoning steps. Existing vision-language models (VLMs) struggle to perform this process reliably. Agentic systems address this challenge by querying external vision and geometry tools, but training their reasoning leaves these tools unchanged, so inaccurate observations can still lead to incorrect answers. We introduce EgoP$^{2}$C, a framework that internalizes a plan-perceive-compose process within a single VLM. To this end, we first train specialized Planner and Perception teachers using separate adapters (Dual-LoRA), then fine-tune the corresponding experts in a mixture-of-experts (MoE) student under their supervision. We subsequently train the student on trajectories generated by its current policy using a segment-level extension of multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD), assigning different trajectory segments to different supervisors. The Planner teacher supervises which evidence-seeking questions to ask, when to stop gathering evidence, and how to compose the final answer. The Perception teacher supervises the video-grounded answers to those questions. At inference, the student executes this adaptive workflow end-to-end, without external spatial tools. Across three spatial reasoning benchmarks, EgoP$^{2}$C students built from Qwen3-VL-8B and SenseNova-SI-1.3-8B outperform their base models by an average of 9.2 and 7.5 percentage points, respectively. Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are usually chosen from leaderboards, which give one ranking for every application. On questions about objects that a scene suggests but does not contain, however, the model with the lowest error on the POPE hallucination benchmark is not the one with the lowest error on such questions. For yes/no questions about object presence, we show that the best model for a target domain can be predicted without running any candidate on that domain, given 650 to 1,200 labeled target items that calibrate a detector. We measure each model’s reliance on a cue, a feature such as scene context or text in the image, by editing 100 labeled source images so that the cue suggests the wrong answer, and comparing with a matched control edit. Reliance predicts which VLMs are misled by physical sticker attacks (Spearman .93 on nine models not used to design the score) and which hallucinate on unedited COCO photographs, beyond what their tendency to answer yes explains. Shift-Reliance Alignment (SRA) scores a model by its source error on the labeled source questions plus its reliance multiplied by the shift. The shift is the proportion of target questions on which the cue misleads, and a detector estimates it on the unlabeled target images. SRA selects the model with the lowest score. In pre-registered tests on new images where most questions mislead, it lowered the error of the selected model from 30.4% under selection by source error to 18.8% on Objects365 and OpenImages but, against its prediction, not on COCO. Abstract: Existing video multimodal large language models (MLLMs) organize observations primarily in time, but embodied spatial reasoning requires organizing them in space. Consequently, their visual representations can remain locally consistent across nearby frames while lacking a persistent spatial context that is stable across viewpoints and time. We exploit depth and camera pose, which are naturally available to embodied agents, to explicitly establish this spatial correspondence. We introduce Cambrian-BEV, which extends Cambrian-S with a parameter-free geometric tokenization scheme that back-projects visual patch features from each frame into a shared bird’s-eye-view (BEV) coordinate frame and averages features that fall within the same spatial cell. Thus, observations of the same physical location, regardless of viewpoint or time step, are aggregated into a common spatial token. In addition to providing a persistent scene representation, this formulation acts as an efficient compression mechanism: 128 frames containing approximately 100K DINOv3 patch features are consolidated into a fixed 27$\times$27 grid of only 729 BEV tokens, cutting LLM prefill time by $2.9\times$ and peak memory by $3.6\times$. The retained tokens should remain close to the dominant visual evidence subspace induced by the full token set. To this end, we propose \emph{Subspace-Transition Token Pruning} (\method{}), which estimates each token’s spectral support from principal component space and constructs a compact representation subspace. Abstract: In autonomous driving, perceiving surrounding objects is not enough: failing to understand how they move and interact over time can lead to dangerous decisions. Evaluating vision-language models (VLMs) therefore requires testing whether they connect past spatial observations to future consequences. Yet existing benchmarks often assess edge cases through basic perception questions or test temporal understanding in simple scenarios, leaving complex spatiotemporal reasoning underexplored. We introduce STRIDE (SpatioTemporal Reasoning In Driving Edge Cases), an object-centric benchmark for long-tail, safety-critical driving scenarios. STRIDE covers six task families: spatial perception, spatial understanding, temporal memory, temporal extrapolation, trajectory prediction, and scene-context awareness. It pairs multiple-choice questions probing individual spatiotemporal judgments with related free-form visual question answering (VQA) requiring detailed scene explanations and justifications of driving decisions. We prioritize scenes with unusual object behaviors and complex traffic interactions, generate reference answers from structured dataset annotations through automated procedures, and use limited language-model assistance and human verification to ensure annotation quality. Across 12 proprietary, open-weight, and driving-specialized models, even the best performer, GPT-6-Astra, achieves only 43.4% average multiple-choice accuracy, versus an approximately 20% random-choice baseline. STRIDE thus exposes spatiotemporal driving limitations that aggregate VQA scores can conceal.

Abstract: Blindness and low vision limit many people’s ability to complete daily tasks, motivating AI assistants that provide useful guidance. Result: 作者分析发现，两类问题的偏差机制不同：计数所需的数量信息对应多模态特征特定维度的幅值，因此计数偏差主要从视觉侧产生并经模态融合传播；非计数偏差主要来自语言侧，也受少量视觉偏差影响。VDB通过不同视觉与语言贡献比例提取双模态偏差，使用基于变分自编码器原理的偏差模型学习以语言为主、带少量视觉影响的偏差，并另建同时融合语言和视觉的双模态偏差模型。在VQACPv2、VQACPv1和GQAOOD上的实验支持作者的偏差解释及方法有效性；论文还分析了VQAv2上的性能折中。

Abstract: Visual question answering (VQA) models are known to suffer from the bias issue, which refers to overfitting the regularities of frequent patterns. Previously, researchers only consider the language bias. Recently, the multimodal bias is noticed. However, what multimodal bias is remains unclear. In this paper, we investigate the multimodal bias in counting and non-counting questions. We find that they have distinct underlying mechanism, so their biases come from distinct reasons. We provide valuable interpretations of VQA counting mechanism: \textbf{quantity information stems from the magnitude of the multimodal features in specific dimensions.} Thus, counting bias stems from the vision side and propagates to multimodal features through modality fusion, while the non-counting bias stems from primarily language bias and slightly vision bias. Thus, we propose a method to deal with them differently: Variational and Decomposed method for mitigating Bimodal Bias (VDB). It extracts bimodal bias affected by different proportion of vision and language sides. We introduce variational bias models utilizing the principle of Variational Auto-Encoder to learn mainly language bias with slight vision effect, and a bimodal bias model for bimodal bias combining language and vision sides. Extensive experiments on VQACPv2, VQACPv1, and GQAOOD datasets demonstrate the rationality of our claim and the effectiveness of our method. We also provide explanation for the compromised performance on VQAv2.

Details

Motivation: 现有嵌入模型擅长全局视频检索，但单一表示会混合多个物体及其时空动态，限制对象级文本定位。需要直接学习文本与具体物体轨迹之间的细粒度对应关系。

Result: VTrajEmbed使用双分支编码：密集采样低分辨率帧捕捉高频运动，稀疏选择高分辨率帧保留场景上下文，再由共享LLM骨干聚合。掩码引导对比目标结合组内损失和批次级双向损失，排除未经核实的关系，并避免把已确认匹配当成负例。作者还构建Text2Traj-Bench并经人工核验查询与轨迹关系。VTrajEmbed-5B的文本到轨迹及轨迹到文本R@1分别比Qwen3-VL-Embedding-8B高15.48和18.05个百分点；五个全局检索基准的平均R@1为49.48%，对照为42.86%。

Abstract: Fine-grained alignment between text and video object trajectories is a core challenge in multimodal understanding. Existing embedding models excel at global retrieval, yet their single global representation entangles multiple objects and their spatiotemporal dynamics, limiting direct object-level grounding. To address this issue, we propose VTrajEmbed, an embedding model that decomposes a video into object trajectories and aligns each trajectory embedding with text for object-level grounding through retrieval. Our main contributions: (1)Slow-Fast Trajectory Encoding, a dual-branch architecture that establishes trajectory representations by densely sampling low-resolution frames for high-frequency motion cues and sparsely selecting high-resolution frames for scene context, aggregated by a shared LLM backbone; (2)Mask-Guided Contrastive Learning, a training objective built upon these representations that couples an intra-group loss and a batch-level bidirectional loss under a relation mask, excluding unverified relations and preventing confirmed matches from becoming false negatives; (3)Text2Traj-Bench, a trajectory-level text-video benchmark with model-assisted construction and human-verified query–trajectory relations. Experimental results show that VTrajEmbed-5B improves text-to-trajectory and trajectory-to-text R@1 over Qwen3-VL-Embedding-8B by 15.48 and 18.05 percentage points, respectively, and achieves 49.48% average R@1 across five global retrieval benchmarks, compared with 42.86% for Qwen3-VL-Embedding-8B.

Details

Motivation: 视觉Transformer的token合并可通过压缩冗余特征降低计算量，但按特征相似度合并在实例分割中可能把相邻、同类别的不同对象融合，破坏实例边界。

Result: MaskMerge使用粗略中间掩码预测限制基于特征的合并，从而保留实例边界。在PanNuke上，相较ALGM，mAP从30.1升至32.1，GFLOPs从229降到153，速度提升1.18倍；在iSAID上，mAP从33.1升至36.2，GFLOPs从198降到182，速度提升1.23倍。在Cityscapes上，方法以更低FLOPs匹配ALGM表现；收益也迁移到MAE预训练骨干。

Abstract: Token merging improves the efficiency of Vision Transformers by combining redundant tokens, but existing methods largely rely on feature similarity to determine which tokens to merge. This criterion is effective for semantic segmentation, yet can fail in instance segmentation, where adjacent objects of the same class often have highly similar features. We identify this failure mode as cross-instance token fusion. We introduce MaskMerge, an instance-aware token merging method that uses coarse intermediate mask predictions to constrain feature-based merging and preserve instance boundaries. We evaluate MaskMerge on two diverse instance segmentation domains characterized by high inter-instance visual similarity: PanNuke and iSAID. Against the SOTA token merging method ALGM, MaskMerge improves mAP from 30.1 to 32.1 on PanNuke while reducing computation from 229 to 153 GFLOPs and running 1.18x faster, and from 33.1 to 36.2 on iSAID while reducing computation from 198 to 182 GFLOPs and running 1.23x faster. Additionally, we also test it on Cityscapes and match ALGM’s performance while using fewer FLOPs. The gains also transfer to an MAE pretrained backbone, demonstrating that instance-aware merging enables more aggressive compression when appearance similarity alone cannot reliably preserve object identity.

[57] COMET: Decoupled Distillation, Routing, and Capacity Control for Task-Agnostic Continual Vision–Language Learning cs.CV | cs.AIPDF

Seungyoon Woo, Keonhee Park, Gunhee Kim

TL;DR: COMET通过特征蒸馏、专家协调、路由和容量控制，应对视觉语言模型在无任务标识的持续学习中的模态漂移、表示干扰与专家容量不足。

Details

Motivation: 视觉语言模型如CLIP具备零样本识别能力，但开放环境持续学习要求任务依次到来、无法访问旧数据、不知道当前任务身份且不能重训骨干。除了稳定性与可塑性的权衡，模态关系漂移、表示空间干扰和每任务容量不足也会造成性能退化。

Result: COMET以冻结教师在共享图文池上提供特征参考；三亲和蒸馏保持图像-图像、文本-文本及图像-文本几何关系；学生感知的跨专家蒸馏让新专家对齐兼容旧专家，使路由错误时性能退化更平滑。方法将容量管理与推理路由分开：上下文赌博机根据训练后信号保留或合并完整任务专家，重建式路由器在测试时选择稀疏专家且无需教师语义。在X-TAIL基准上，消融显示各组件均能改善持续学习表现。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) such as CLIP retain zero-shot recognition ability, but their open-world use often requires task-agnostic continual learning: tasks arrive sequentially, past data are unavailable, task identity is unknown, and the backbone cannot be retrained. We argue that this setting fails VLMs through three coupled pressures beyond the stability-plasticity tradeoff: (1) modal-consistency drift, (2) representation-space interference, and (3) per-task capacity shortfall. We propose COMET (Collaborative Mixture-of-Expert Transfer) to resolve these three issues respectively as follows. A frozen teacher provides only a feature reference on a shared image-text pool; Tri-Affinity Distillation preserves image-image, text-text, and image-text geometry; and Student-Aware Cross-Expert Distillation aligns new experts with compatible prior experts so routing errors degrade gracefully. We design COMET to keep capacity and inference separate: a contextual bandit keeps or merges full per-task experts using post-training signals, while a reconstruction router selects sparse experts at test time without teacher semantics. On the X-TAIL benchmark, we show that each proposed component improves continual learning performance. COMET shows that continual vision-language adaptation can preserve multimodal geometry, coordinate accumulated experts, and allocate capacity jointly when feature reference, expert shaping, routing, and capacity control are separated by construction.

Details

Motivation: 多传感器目标重识别需要融合互补信息，但跨模态对齐可能把共享结构与模态专属线索混在一起，常规融合也未必能充分利用各传感器的独有信息。

Abstract: Multi-modal object re-identification (ReID) leverages complementary information from heterogeneous sensors. Cross-modal alignment and fusion remain challenging. Existing methods mainly reduce modality gaps via feature alignment and fusion. However, alignment may entangle modality-shared structural information with modality-specific cues, while conventional fusion may not fully exploit complementary information. To address these issues, we propose ODSS-Net, a framework for orthogonally decoupled shared-specific representation learning and adaptive fusion of complementary modality-specific features. First, a Shared Structural Feature Construction (SSFC) module suppresses modality-dependent appearance variations and extracts modality-shared structural information. Then, an Orthogonal Specific Feature Learning (OSFL) module aggregates identity-relevant local information and removes the shared-aligned component via geometric orthogonal projection. Finally, a Sample Adaptive Mixture of Experts (SAMoE) module uses the shared structure as routing context to adaptively refine and fuse modality-specific features. Experiments on three benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of ODSS-Net for multi-modal object ReID.

Abstract: Poor illumination and occlusion obscure clothing cues in indoor work sites, while diverse clothing styles complicate workwear compliance judgments.
