Insight: 仅看答案对错会漏掉模型解释与证据不符的问题，ANCHOR提供了分析音视频推理依据的测试平台。参考排名稳定性仅在配对的100段视频子集上验证；模态消融数值也限于所测模型和人工精修测试集。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) perform well on audio-visual question answering, but correct answers do not necessarily imply that their stated reasoning is grounded in relevant perceptual evidence. Existing benchmarks mainly evaluate final-answer accuracy, offering limited insight into whether model-generated rationales correctly identify visual regions, temporal intervals, and audio events supporting predictions. We introduce ANCHOR, a benchmark and evaluation framework for measuring grounded-rationale alignment in multimodal reasoning, comprising 2,319 videos and 8,272 question-answer pairs with structured reasoning traces, temporal evidence, spatial bounding boxes, and salient audio cues, including a human-refined test set of 224 videos and 975 question-answer pairs. We separately measure answer correctness, hallucination, logical divergence, and conciseness, with direct spatial and temporal grounding measurements on a focused subset. Across the full benchmark, twelve contemporary MLLMs show a substantial alignment gap: models with comparable answer performance can differ markedly in whether their explanations are supported by annotated evidence. On a matched subset of 100 videos and 446 question-answer pairs, rankings remain highly stable across human-refined, Gemini-generated, and GPT-generated references (mean Pearson r=0.99), providing evidence against model-reference circularity within this subset. Grounding-cue ablations of three MLLMs on the full human-refined test set reveal a consistent modality hierarchy: removing video reduces accuracy by 38.4-41.8 points, while removing bounding boxes (3.2-7.7 points) and audio (4.2-5.6 points) causes smaller, complementary, model-dependent drops. These findings show answer-only evaluation obscures important grounding failures and position ANCHOR as a testbed for evaluating accurate and evidence-aligned audio-visual reasoning.

Details

Motivation: 图像中值得提取的信息及其需要的token预算取决于具体问题，但多数视觉模型独立于查询编码图像，并为所有输入分配固定视觉token预算，可能浪费计算或遗漏相关证据。

Result: 查询自适应循环分词器QART可用于不同视觉骨干，从冻结编码器特征出发反复构造紧凑表示，并根据查询相关区域的重建质量决定是否继续增加token，直到相关信息保真度足够。在Visual7W和VQAv2上，使用约29个视觉token时准确率分别达到81.96%和76.37%，token数比密集视觉语言模型输入减少20–25倍。在CORE上P@1为92.0%，在PODS上PR-AUC为36.3%，并比SteerViT高8.4%。

Abstract: Visual information worth extracting from an image and the token budget it warrants depend on the text query. Yet modern vision and vision-language models largely encode images independently of the query and allocate a fixed visual token budget to every input. We introduce the Query-Adaptive Recurrent Tokenizer (QART), a backbone-agnostic module that makes visual representations both query-steerable and budget-adaptive. QART steers the visual representation toward query-relevant evidence while adapting its token budget to the amount of information required. Its key principle is simple. Additional tokens are allocated until the visual features relevant to the query are represented with sufficient fidelity. Starting from frozen encoder features, QART recurrently constructs a compact representation and uses reconstruction quality over the query-relevant visual support to control token allocation. On Visual7W and VQAv2, QART achieves 81.96% and 76.37% accuracy using only about 29 visual tokens, reducing token counts by 20-25x relative to dense VLM inputs. It further reaches 92.0% P@1 on CORE and 36.3% PR-AUC on PODS, outperforming SteerViT on PODS by 8.4%. Together, these results show that visual representations need not be fixed in either content or capacity and can instead adapt to what each query requires.

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) have become a common backbone for high-quality text-to-image and text-to-video generation, but repeated dense self-attention remains a major inference bottleneck as token sequences grow and the same operator is invoked across many denoising steps. Our study suggests that dense attention in pretrained DiTs contains reusable layer/head-specific high-mass structure that can be identified from a small offline calibration set and reused across future generations. This leads to Sink-Sparse Hybrid Attention, a train-free attention replacement that reuses static sink masks and supplements them with a lightweight pooled-KV branch, while leaving model weights and sampling schedules unchanged. On Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B, the method attains 1.54$\times$ end-to-end speedup while maintaining generation quality. On Z-Image-Turbo, it attains 1.57$\times$ end-to-end speedup with competitive GenEval2 performance. These results indicate that reusable importance-aware sparse support can provide an effective train-free acceleration path for both T2V and T2I DiTs.

[63] How Murky Before a VLM Knows It Cannot See? Accuracy, Confidence and Abstention on a Physical Visibility Axis cs.CV | cs.LGPDF

Mochamad Rizal Fauzan, Irgi Surya

TL;DR: 该研究以物理可见距离测量雾和水下图像退化，发现多个小型视觉语言模型准确率已降至随机水平时，内部和口头置信度仍可能很高。

Details

Motivation: 鲁棒性研究常用没有物理单位的“等级1–5”表示图像退化，且较少检验模型置信度是否随可见性下降而合理变化。实际场景需要知道模型何时看不清，以及能否可靠选择弃答。

Result: 作者使用Koschmieder模型模拟雾、按纯水吸收谱模拟水下衰减，并基于单目度量深度图，在1,000张COCO场景及五个2–5B参数开源模型上，将可见距离从250米扫到1.95米。框选物体识别准确率在狭窄范围内从接近干净图像水平降至随机水平：雾下半程可见距离为12.1–15.9米，水下为20.5–38.6米。对四个干净图像时较自信的模型，雾下可见距离1.95米时准确率已接近随机，但内部置信度仍为0.68–0.84、口头置信度为0.61–1.00，盲区宽度至少0.79个数量级，即约6.2倍可见距离。明确说明原因的弃答选项更能跟随准确率；泛化的“不知道”对一个模型仍滞后。ImageNet-C雾等级1–5对应55–110米，未进入该失效区间。

Insight: 标准合成退化等级可能远未覆盖模型真正失明的物理范围；要求模型明确说明看不清的原因也可能改善弃答行为。研究为描述性结果，基于一次1,000图像抽样、假定尺度深度及小模型，不能外推至所有真实能见度或大模型。

Abstract: Robustness studies of vision-language models (VLMs) grade degradation on nominal severity scales (“level 1-5”) that carry no physical units, and they rarely ask whether a model’s confidence follows the loss of visibility. We measure accuracy, internal (token-probability) confidence, verbalized confidence and an explicit abstain option on a visibility axis in metres: fog follows the Koschmieder model, an underwater arm scales the absorption spectrum of pure water, both are applied with a monocular metric depth map, and the visibility range V is swept over eight log-spaced levels from 250 m to 1.95 m. On 1000 COCO scenes and five small open VLMs (2-5 B parameters), accuracy on a boxed-object identification task falls from near its clean value to chance within a narrow band (half-way visibility V_acc of 12.1-15.9 m in fog and 20.5-38.6 m under water) while, for the four models that are confident on clean images, the confidence stays high: at V=1.95 m in fog their accuracy is at chance but their mean internal confidence is 0.68-0.84 and their verbalized confidence 0.61-1.00, a blind-spot width W=log10(V_acc/V_aware) of at least 0.79 decades, i.e. a factor of 6.2 in visibility. The remaining model is under-confident throughout and its internal confidence follows accuracy. An explicit abstain option follows accuracy when it names the reason (W from -0.21 to -0.06 in fog) but, for one model, lags with a generic “I don’t know” (W up to 0.18). ImageNet-C fog severities 1-5 correspond to only 55-110 m of physical visibility, outside the failure band, and leave the accuracy of the models within 5 percentage points of its clean value. Blank-image and shuffled-image controls, a prior-matched answer set and a 200-image pilot that preceded the study are reported. Findings are descriptive: one draw of 1000 images, depth of assumed scale, and models of 2-5 B parameters.

Abstract: MRI field-strength enhancement seeks to combine the accessibility of low-field scanners with the image quality of high-field scanners. Yet, existing methods remain prone to anatomical hallucinations, as uniform processing leaves difficult brain regions insufficiently examined and scarce paired data limit their learning of how tissue contrast and anatomical visibility change across field strengths. To address these challenges, we propose UniField++, a two-agent framework that unifies enhancement tasks across multiple field-strength transitions, including 64mT-to-3T and 3T-to-7T. Unified learning allows both the VLM-based planner and the flow-matching-based enhancer to draw on field-dependent image characteristics observed across tasks, rather than relying on a single transition. Trained with radiologists’ annotations of hallucination-prone regions and corresponding correction plans, the planner predicts regional risk and formulates targeted restoration strategies. Guided by these plans, the enhancer performs loop reasoning along the flow-matching trajectory, repeatedly processing high-risk regions for additional refinement. Together, cross-transition learning and risk-guided refinement support anatomically faithful enhancement across field strengths. Across the evaluated tasks, UniField++ improves PSNR by an average of 2.74 dB and SSIM by 14.05 percentage points over comparison methods, with corresponding gains of 3.97 dB and 13.92 percentage points over the ablation baseline. It also maintains enhancement quality on an external cohort with an unseen field strength and improves downstream whole-brain segmentation Macro Dice by an average of 0.1457 over the original low-field images. Code is available at https://github.com/CUHK-AIM-Group/UniField_plusplus.

Abstract: The use of accurate vision models for crop monitoring, disease diagnosis, and plant phenotyping in agriculture is hindered by the lack of expertly annotated data, especially in developing countries. A100 uses a hybrid CNN-Transformer backbone that fuses MBConv stages for local feature extraction with Transformer stages for global contextual representation. A100 uses pre-training with the integration of DINO self-distillation, iBOT masked patch reconstruction, and KoLeo uniformity regularization, resulting in features that are better than ImageNet-12K supervised representations for agricultural applications without any fine-tuning. By utilizing a frozen backbone, the accuracy for crop classification of A100 is 95.37%, which is +2.56% better than ImageNet-12K, and reduces the leaf count error by 10.4% on the GroMo25 phenotyping benchmark, proving that the same backbone can be used for classification and regression tasks. Insight: 保留分支级证据控制能力能让跟踪器在单一模态暂时不可靠时调整依赖来源。摘要没有给出各场景、失效类型和基线绝对分数；代码计划在接收后发布，当前复现信息有限。

Abstract: Recent RGB-infrared multi-object tracking (MOT) methods have substantially improved cross-modal representation learning and multimodal information integration. However, most existing methods compress RGB and infrared evidence into a unified descriptor. When one modality is temporarily degraded, the tracker can no longer regulate its contribution separately; unreliable evidence may distort association and contaminate appearance memory. We therefore propose RCA-MOT, a reliability-consistent appearance modeling framework that preserves cross-modal complementarity while retaining post-representation evidence controllability. RGB-specific, infrared-specific, and fused identity representations are maintained as jointly accessible appearance states. For each detection, a simple training-free target-scene estimator produces branch-wise appearance reliability used across the two temporal roles of appearance: relative reliability regulates different identity branches, absolute reliability calibrates borderline candidates near the ReID acceptance boundary, and the same branch-wise reliability controls how strongly matched descriptors update historical appearance memory. The individual components are intentionally simple, focusing evaluation on reliability-controllable appearance organization rather than elaborate fusion or learned reliability-estimation modules. RCA-MOT sets new SOTA results on VT-MOT and UniRTL, improving the previous best HOTA by 5.402/6.075 points and IDF1 by 11.549/12.789 points, respectively, while post-hoc analyses further validate reliability as an informative control signal. Code will be released upon acceptance.

Abstract: Generative models for deepfakes have rapidly evolved from GAN-based architectures to diffusion models and multimodal large language models (MLLMs). However, empirical research on human performance in detecting deepfake face images has not kept pace. It remains unclear if (and how) detection performance varies across generative models and whether model-specific training improves detection more effectively than general-purpose training. In this paper, we propose the \emph{Deepfake Face Artifact Taxonomy} (DFAT), a cross-model taxonomy of human-recognizable visual artifacts in deepfake face images, based on which we build both general-purpose and model-specific training materials. Through an online experiment ($n = 524$), we examined human performance in detecting deepfake face images and evaluated training efficacy across five representative generative models: three GAN-based, one diffusion-based, and one MLLM (GPT-5). Our results show that participants’ detection accuracy was strongly model-dependent and that images generated by more recent and advanced models were not necessarily harder to detect. After training, participants achieved significant performance gains across most conditions, and their improvement varied by model. Notably, model-specific training generally yielded greater numerical gains than general-purpose training. In a five-month follow-up test ($n = 342$), we observed no significant decline in detection accuracy and no significant increase in perceived stress. Overall, our findings provide actionable insights for designing effective, sustainable, and targeted training programs to counter the growing threat posed by deepfakes.

Abstract: Zero-shot anomaly detection (ZSAD) aims to detect anomalies in previously unseen classes using auxiliary images, with applications such as industrial visual inspection and medical imaging. Recent vision-language-model-based approaches improve ZSAD by aligning textual and visual representations, while distributional prompting further models prompt variability for unseen categories. However, existing methods do not explicitly combine normality-referenced distributional modeling with hierarchy-specific prompt specialization, limiting their ability to model anomalous semantics relative to normality while addressing both image-level detection and fine-grained localization. We propose Hierarchical Knowledge-Guided Distributional Prompt Learning (HiDist), a normality-referenced hierarchical distributional prompt framework for ZSAD. HiDist models each prompt token with a diagonal Gaussian distribution and generates stochastic prompt variations through Monte Carlo sampling. Rather than learning normal and anomalous distributions independently, anomalous prompt distributions are parameterized as deviations from learned normal references, explicitly encoding their dependency. The resulting distributions are specialized into global and local representations for image-level detection and pixel-level localization, respectively, while an adapted global visual representation guides hierarchical alignment. Experiments on seven industrial and medical datasets demonstrate strong cross-dataset performance across anomaly detection and localization tasks. Extensive ablation and representation analyses further support the effectiveness of the proposed distributional modeling and hierarchy-specific representation learning.

Abstract: This paper presents the overview of the runs related to the Ad-hoc Video Search (AVS) and Video Question Answering (VQA) tracks of TRECVID 2025 on behalf of the CERTH-ITI team. For the AVS track, we introduce a two-stage framework built on foundation models. In the first stage, multiple vision-language models (VLMs) encode both the input query, augmented through LLM-generated rephrasings, and the candidate video shots, producing weighted similarity scores for initial retrieval. In the second stage, we utilize a Multimodal-LLM(MLLM)-based reranking module that evaluates the semantic alignment between each shot among the top-N highest-ranked ones and the original query, generating updated relevance scores for reordering these shots. This MLLM-driven reranking significantly improves contextual matching and produces more accurate final rankings without requiring any model training. Regarding the VQA track, we fine-tune an audio-visual MLLM model on the provided TRECVID training dataset and we implement an inference-time scaling technique to enhance the multimodal understanding capabilities of the MLLM. For the open-ended Answer Generation (AG) task, we aggregate multiple model responses per question via a majority vote. The responses are generated with greedy sampling from different random frame subsets of the video and they are ranked based on the number of votes. For the Multiple-Choice (MC) task, instead of voting, we use mean pooling on the logits assigned by the fine-tuned model to each candidate response. Through the combination of fine-tuning and frame subset ensembling we achieve the highest score across 3 metrics in the VQA AG task and the second highest in the VQA MC task.

Abstract: Jointly reconstructing RGB and X-ray provides new insights by connecting external appearance with internal structure, enabling a more complete understanding of real-world objects than single modality. Yet existing methods train separate models per modality and suffer from redundant geometry, alignment artifacts, and loose coupling that limits cross-modal insight discovery. We propose a unified representation in which all Gaussian primitives share a single set of geometric parameters, with only surface-proximal primitives additionally carrying appearance attributes. We perform cross-modal registration through clean multi-view stereo and X-ray geometric proxies instead of aligning two sets of optimized Gaussians, improving alignment stability. We then jointly optimize the unified representation via supervision from both modalities, complemented by a surface-depth consistency loss derived from X-ray geometry to guide RGB reconstruction. Experiments on the InsideOut dataset and our Flower & Insect dataset show that our framework achieves more robust cross-modal alignment, higher RGB rendering quality without compromising X-ray reconstruction, and a more compact representation than maintaining two independent geometries.

[73] Can the Parts Fool the Test? Counterfactual Pairing Cycles for Relational OOD cs.CV | cs.LGPDF

Yibo Zhou, Yirong Xiang

TL;DR: 反事实配对循环通过固定两张图和两条文本、仅交换图文配对，构造更干净的关系分布外评测，并可用于训练和诊断视觉语言模型。

Details

Motivation: 关系型分布外错误难以干净评测：图像、文本本身都自然常见，但文本关系可能描述错图像。许多负例仍可通过文风、模板、长度、物体频率或图像统计差异识别，无需真正检查图文配对关系。

Result: 作者提出成组反事实配对循环：两张图像和两条文本在比较双方保持完全相同，只改变图文匹配组合，因此有限样本中仅依赖图像、仅依赖文本或可加性成分的捷径会精确抵消。这种对比还可构成关系型异常暴露训练损失，并诊断双线性视觉语言评分。在合成控制、GQA和COCO实验中，循环能区分粗粒度图文匹配与方向角色绑定错误，也显示几何尺寸关系仍可通过全局特征识别。在带捷径污染的COCO异常暴露压力测试中，基于近乎完美捷径验证AUROC选出的模型虽保留关系信号，但不如循环训练；CycleAcc经循环训练从0.882提升到0.927。

Insight: 若要测试模型是否理解图文配对关系，保持图像和文本组成部分不变、只改变配对，可消除多类捷径。循环训练仍不能证明模型对所有关系都具备因果理解；摘要未说明全部数据规模和不同关系类型的逐项结果。

Abstract: Relational out-of-distribution errors are easy to describe and hard to evaluate cleanly. An image may be natural, a caption may be fluent, and all mentioned objects may be common, yet the caption may describe the wrong relation in the image. Many evaluations intended to test this behavior can be solved without checking the pairing at all, because negatives differ in caption style, template, length, object frequency, or image statistics. We propose counterfactual pairing cycles, a blocked evaluation primitive that reuses the same two images and the same two captions on both sides of the comparison, changing only which image is paired with which caption. This makes image-only, text-only, and additive component shortcuts cancel exactly in finite samples. The same contrast also gives a training loss for relational outlier exposure and a diagnostic for bilinear vision-language scores. Across synthetic controls, GQA, and COCO, cycles separate coarse image-caption matching from directional role-binding failures, while also identifying geometric size relations that remain strongly visible in global features. In a shortcut-contaminated COCO outlier-exposure stress test, a model selected by a near-perfect shortcut validation AUROC retains substantial relational signal but remains below cycle training on exact cycles; cycle training improves CycleAcc from 0.882 to 0.927. The main lesson is simple: to test whether a model understands a pairing, the benchmark should keep the parts fixed, change the pairing, and ask only about coupling. The official implementation is available online.

Abstract: Making a conference poster is visual coding in miniature: an entire paper must be compressed onto a single page, which exercises summarization, visual design, and code implementation at once, using nothing beyond the paper itself. Yet benchmarks for this task in the context of visual coding remain scarce: existing resources are small and annotated only with layout boxes, so evaluation must fall back on VLM-as-judge or human checklists rather than objective render-and-compare, and the generation methods they support design layouts from scratch, leaving human-crafted posters unusable as either a retrievable design library or as verified supervision. We introduce RAP (Reconstruct A Poster), a dataset and benchmark for paper-to-poster generation as a visual coding task. RAP contains 25K+ verified paper-poster pairs from various machine learning conferences held between 2022 and 2026, annotated with a three-level content taxonomy, plus a held-out benchmark of 150 test papers each paired with its human poster. A fully automated derendering pipeline turns each poster into styled HTML/CSS of typed, refillable slots, so every sample is a paper-poster-markup triplet verifiable by rendering against the source poster. The corpus serves both inference and training: PosterRAG, a training-free multi-agent pipeline, retrieves an exemplar and refills its slots without touching layout or styling, while an SFT+RL recipe trains a dedicated model from the same triplets. Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) answer questions about whole images well, yet they still struggle with fine-grained region understanding. A key reason is that text-only supervision never specifies which regions support an answer. To close this gap, we present Xiaomi-Lens-0, a 4B MLLM built on Qwen3.5 that predicts masks from its own visual representations. Specifically, a lightweight decoder combines vision-encoder patch features with visual latents, the language model’s mask-token states, so mask supervision reaches the shared backbone without an additional segmentation encoder. Because each latent decodes to a mask, the model can interleave reasoning with region-specific latents and thus think with visual latents, while grounded reasoning traces supervise the regions it visits during chain-of-thought. As a result, across all eight RefCOCO, RefCOCO+, and RefCOCOg splits, Xiaomi-Lens-0 gains 5.6 points on average over Qwen3.5 in referring comprehension and achieves the highest segmentation cIoU among compared models. Motivation: 前馈高斯重建可从少量图像快速预测三维场景，但扩展到长视频会遇到两类瓶颈：同时处理所有视角占用大量显存；累积逐像素预测又会重复表示同一表面，增加存储和渲染开销。

Abstract: Reconstructing high-quality, renderable 3D scenes from long videos is essential for robotic perception and embodied intelligence. Many feed-forward Gaussian reconstruction methods rapidly predict 3D scenes from a small set of images, but extending them to longer sequences presents two bottlenecks: jointly processing all views increases GPU memory requirements, while accumulating pixel-aligned predictions redundantly represents the same surfaces and increases storage and rendering costs. We present LonGaussian, a streaming feed-forward framework that reconstructs compact, globally renderable Gaussian scenes from long image sequences with known intrinsics and unknown camera poses. Built on a pretrained streaming 3D reconstruction backbone, LonGaussian decodes long-context geometric features into dense Gaussians. Within each chunk, multi-frame point tracks link repeated observations of the same surface and guide a learned merger to consolidate the corresponding Gaussian predictions. Rendered-depth consistency filtering further suppresses Gaussians from other chunks that incorrectly occlude locally reconstructed surfaces. In this work, we revisit I2V immunization as reducing source-content preservation, shifting the focus from output degradation to weakening source-conditioned content propagation. To this end, we propose I2V-DETACH, an image immunization method that prevents unauthorized I2V generation through source grounding detachment. I2V models preserve source content by propagating source-image cues into video latents during denoising. Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) repeatedly process hundreds of visual tokens across decoder layers, incurring substantial computation and memory costs. Existing inference-time visual token pruning methods often irreversibly remove low-ranked tokens, although the set of visual tokens receiving high query-conditioned attention can change across decoder depth. Recoverable routing preserves skipped tokens for later reconsideration, but re-admits them with stale representations after bypassing intermediate transformations. We further find that replacing stale re-admitted states with their current-depth oracle states from a full-token reference trajectory substantially improves grounding performance. We therefore propose CatchUp, a training-free framework for depth-aware visual-token re-admission. CatchUp repeatedly re-evaluates query-conditioned visual relevance, processes only selected visual tokens through each decoder interval, and preserves deferred tokens for later re-admission. Upon re-admission, CatchUp approximates missed representation evolution using reference-to-current transition signals from semantically and spatially related visual tokens. Across three referring-expression grounding datasets and six multimodal benchmarks, CatchUp achieves the highest average relative retention in both benchmark groups at 64-, 128-, and 192-token budgets. At a 64-token budget with 88.9% fewer active visual tokens, CatchUp retains 92.17% of the full model’s performance on average across six multimodal benchmarks while reducing end-to-end latency by 8.0%. Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have significantly advanced instruction-driven perception through the “Embedding-as-Mask” paradigm. We identify a critical yet underexplored bottleneck: the sequential alienation of the segmentation token [SEG] from its initial visual grounding within the autoregressive generation paradigm. Produced only at the end of long reasoning chains, [SEG] relies on visual evidence that has been progressively diluted by linguistic abstraction and autoregressive noise. This information decay is particularly catastrophic for RS targets with minuscule pixel footprints, where even marginal fidelity loss leads to localization failure. To address these bottlenecks, we propose GeoReason, a novel framework designed to fortify logical deduction and preserve spatial integrity. Extensive experiments demonstrate that GeoReason consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods on the Earthreason and RRSIS-D benchmarks. TL;DR: ECDD把“正确答案是否依赖相关图像证据”纳入医学视觉语言模型蒸馏目标，在保持原图答题准确率的同时增强模型对证据移除的敏感性。

Details

Motivation: 轻量医学视觉语言模型可能在没有真正利用图像的情况下答对问题，而依赖题目措辞或训练先验。普通知识蒸馏只复制教师在原图上的答案偏好，不检查这一偏好是否建立在问题所需视觉证据之上。

Result: 证据条件依赖蒸馏ECDD将训练图像与证据水平匹配的控制图像配对，蒸馏教师在两者间答案margin的下降；下界目标只惩罚证据依赖不足，同时用原图监督保持任务准确率。该框架可用于指定临床区域、手术器械或全图内容等证据。在三个医学VQA设置中，ECDD保持了与蒸馏及对齐基线相近的原图准确率，同时让答案支持度更敏感于移除相关证据。在DME主要指标下，针对目标区域而非随机区域遮挡时的正例margin响应从KD的0.786提高到2.023，原图准确率仅相差0.11个百分点。

Insight: 医学模型蒸馏应传递教师答案偏好，也要传递该偏好对视觉证据的依赖，避免学到只凭先验答题的捷径。摘要未展开DME指标定义和三个设置的全部数值，结果限于所测证据遮挡方案。

Abstract: Compact medical vision-language models (VLMs) can answer clinical questions correctly without reliably using the image, relying instead on question wording or learned priors. Standard knowledge distillation copies a teacher’s answer preference on the original image, but it leaves unchecked whether that preference is conditional on the visual evidence required by the question. We study this missing target as medical visual evidence dependency: support for the correct answer should decrease when the matched evidence is absent. We introduce Evidence-Conditioned Dependency Distillation (ECDD), which pairs each training image with a control matched to the evidence level and distills the teacher’s drop in answer margin across the pair. A lower bound objective transfers positive dependency by penalizing only shortfalls, while original-image supervision preserves task accuracy. The same formulation applies when the evidence is a queried clinical region, a surgical instrument, or whole-image visual content. Across three medical VQA settings, ECDD keeps original-image accuracy competitive with distillation and alignment baselines while making answer support more sensitive to evidence removal. Under the DME headline objective, for example, the positive-case margin response to target rather than random masking rises from 0.786 with KD to 2.023 with ECDD, while original-image accuracy differs by 0.11 points. These results suggest that medical VLM distillation should transfer both the teacher’s answer preference and its dependence on visual evidence.

[86] ReVision: Reprogramming Image Generators for Dense Visual Perception cs.CVPDF

Yitong Yang, Yinglin Wang, Xuexin Liu, Wei Chen, Zhaoyang Xu

TL;DR: ReVision根据每个图像及自然语言指令动态生成临时低秩参数更新，让冻结的图像生成器执行分割、深度和法线等稠密视觉预测。

Details

Motivation: 图像生成模型拥有丰富视觉先验，可用于稠密感知；但把多个感知任务编码到共享参数中进行联合训练，容易受异质数据混合及权重影响，并引发任务间干扰。

Result: ReVision由冻结的多模态理解模型和冻结图像生成器组成。理解模型联合编码图像及自由形式指令，可训练参数生成器再将其映射为图像和任务条件化的临时低秩更新；不同图像—指令对会产生不同更新，无需任务专属输出头、显式任务ID或保存适配器。分割、深度估计和表面法线预测通过共享RGB输出接口统一。在多种稠密预测基准上，ReVision达到与先进方法有竞争力的表现，同时支持自然语言灵活指定任务。分析显示任务间存在明显梯度冲突；生成更新也呈现有结构的任务和输入相关权重组织。

Insight: 按实例生成短暂参数更新可避免不同任务长期争用同一套静态参数，也可用自然语言统一任务接口。摘要未给出各任务分项结果、参数生成开销和生成器具体实现，竞争力结论不能等同于全面超过专用模型。

Abstract: Recent image generators exhibit rich visual priors that can support dense visual perception. However, existing approaches typically encode multiple perception tasks into a shared parameterization, making joint training sensitive to the mixture and weighting of heterogeneous data and prone to cross-task interference. To address this issue, we introduce ReVision, an instruction-conditioned parameter generation framework built on a frozen multimodal understanding model and a frozen image generator. Given an image and a free-form instruction, the understanding model jointly encodes them into a multimodal image–instruction representation, which a trainable parameter generator maps to transient low-rank updates for the generator. Each image–instruction pair induces a distinct set of transient prediction parameters, enabling instance-specific adaptation while both pretrained backbones remain unchanged. ReVision requires no task-specific heads, explicit task identifiers, or stored adapters. A shared RGB output interface unifies segmentation, depth estimation, and surface-normal prediction. Across diverse dense prediction benchmarks, ReVision achieves competitive performance with state-of-the-art methods while supporting flexible task specification through natural-language instructions. Our analysis further reveals substantial gradient disagreement across tasks, highlighting the conflict introduced when heterogeneous objectives share a single parameterization. Meanwhile, the generated updates exhibit structured task- and input-dependent organization in weight space, further validating instance-level parameter generation for multi-task perception.

[87] Execution Before Memory: Diagnosing Memory Failures in Video World Models cs.CV | cs.CLPDF

Weiyang Jin, Jiwen Yu, Sihan Xu, Yuwei Niu, Xihui Liu

TL;DR: MIST把视频世界模型记忆评测中的场景设置、执行指令变化和最终呈现结果分开，发现主要瓶颈常是模型没执行变化；M3-Agent通过递归改写指令提高执行成功率。

Details

Motivation: 常见视频世界模型记忆测试把场景是否设置、指令变化是否执行及最后一帧是否显示结果混为一个分数。若模型没执行要求的改变，隐藏状态转移便不会出现，低分不能简单归因为遗忘。

Result: MIST先让模型设置场景但隐藏所要求的变化，之后才要求展示变化，并控制此前信息泄漏，从而区分记忆与执行。评测发现模型能设置场景，却常未落实指令变化；一般记忆基准也出现同样差距。基于此，M3-Agent递归修订输入指令细节，使冻结模型更成功地执行所需变化。相较基线，MIST reveal分数提高到三倍以上；WRBench的T分数提高26.1%，MBench-T的U分数提高52.7%。

Insight: 在归咎于记忆失败前，应确认模型确实执行了预期状态变化；分离执行和保留可提供更严格诊断。M3-Agent通过澄清式指令修订提升执行，但摘要未给出各模型绝对分数、修订成本及不同任务的收益。

Abstract: A memory test for a video world model should say whether the scene was set, whether the asked change was carried out, and whether the last frame shows the result. Current tests fold these into one number, so a low score is easy to read as forgetting. When the change is not carried out, the hidden state transition cannot appear, so the test is not really a test of memory. We introduce MIST, an evaluation that separates the change from the memory it should leave behind. The model first sets the scene while the change stays hidden, and only later is asked to show it, with leakage before that point kept under control. This separation locates the bottleneck: the model sets the scene but does not carry out the instructed change. The same gap appears in general memory evaluations. On that basis we propose M3-Agent (Make the Missing Move), which recursively revises the details of the input instruction so that the model successfully carries out the requested instruction. Relative to the baseline, it more than triples the MIST reveal score. On the benchmarks, WRBench’s score T rises by 26.1% and MBench-T’s score U by 52.7%. The method meets the problem from the execution side: it shows what the frozen model can do once the move is stated, and it sets a stricter baseline.

[88] LiveSync: Iterative Self-Training for Lip-Sync Generation cs.CVPDF

Huaishao Luo, Huan Zhang, Guanbo Bao, Yinglu Liu, Youzheng Wu

TL;DR: LiveSync通过冻结模型生成口型编辑伪配对，再用筛选后的样本迭代微调同一模型，改善音频驱动唇形同步且不增加推理阶段。

Details

Motivation: 音频驱动口型同步需要让嘴和下颌局部动作匹配目标语音，同时保持人物身份、头部姿态和周边面部上下文。真实同步视频提供重建目标，却很少有身份与姿态一致、仅语音相关口型变化不同的配对；随机参考训练输入也与推理时对齐的自参考不一致。

Result: LiveSync先在精选真实视频语料上训练单个掩码式模型，并加入外部生成片段覆盖稀缺视觉条件。冻结检查点使用独立采样音频编辑真实视频帧的嘴部，掩码外像素从原帧复制；置信筛选后，将这些参考与对齐的掩码输入及原始真实帧目标配对。筛选得到的伪样本和真实训练数据混合，用当前权重继续训练同一模型，不增加推理阶段。以单次前向GAN模型实例化后，LiveSync在HDTF上达到领先同步表现，在AIGC-LipSync子集上相较基线有竞争力，且推理延迟较低。

Insight: 用推理形态相似的自参考伪配对更新训练监督，可减少训练与部署输入不匹配；同一思路理论上也可迁移到扩散模型，但文中实例是GAN。摘要未提供同步指标、延迟数值和伪配对筛选率。

Abstract: Audio-driven lip synchronization aims to match target speech through localized mouth and jaw articulation while preserving subject identity, head pose, and surrounding facial context. Naturally recorded videos provide synchronized reconstruction targets but rarely offer pairs that are matched in identity and pose yet differ primarily in speech-related articulation. Mask-based training enables reconstruction from these videos, but random-reference inputs differ from the aligned self-reference used at inference. We present LiveSync, an iterative self-training framework in which a single mask-based model generates pseudo pairs and is refined for deployment. The model is first trained on a curated real-video corpus augmented with externally generated clips covering under-represented visual conditions. A frozen checkpoint then uses independently sampled audio to generate mouth-edited references from real-video frames, copying pixels outside the edit mask from each source frame. After confidence filtering, these references are paired with aligned masked inputs and original real-frame targets. The retained pseudo pairs are mixed with real training data to refine the same model from its current weights, updating training supervision without adding inference stages. Although we instantiate the framework with a single-pass GAN-based model, the self-training strategy could in principle also be adapted to diffusion-based models. Experimental results show that LiveSync achieves leading synchronization performance on HDTF and competitive performance compared with the baselines on the AIGC-LipSync subset, while maintaining low inference latency.

Details

Motivation: 像素空间端到端生成无需学习图像tokenizer，也已获得有竞争力表现；但像素误差是否充分表达语义监督，以及语言模型相关信号能否提供更丰富的图像生成训练目标，仍值得研究。

Result: LILIE是语言感知且无损可逆的图像编码变换，训练目标是让视觉语言模型能从加噪特征恢复图像描述。编码器采用归一化流Transformer，并与视觉语言模型通过下一token预测联合训练。随后将该编码器提取为文生图训练损失。用LILIE损失训练JiT模型后，在GPIC数据集上gFD较纯像素损失基线降低14点，从66.84降至53.01。

Abstract: End-to-end visual generation with pixel-space loss has recently achieved competitive performance without the need for learned image tokenizers. In this work, we study whether language-informed supervision can provide a richer training signal than pixel loss for visual generation. We introduce LILIE (pronounced “lily”), a Language-Informed and Lossless Image Encoder. LILIE is an invertible image transform trained such that a vision-language model can recover captions from noised features. The encoder uses a normalizing flow transformer trained jointly with a vision-language model via next token prediction. We then use the encoder to extract a simple language-informed loss for text-to-image generation. [93] Not Every Mention Is a Claim: Rethinking Object Hallucination Detection in Vision-Language Models cs.CV | cs.CLPDF

Hsin-Ling Hsu, Neeraja Kirtane, Kuan-Hao Huang

TL;DR: NAME-Bench区分描述中“提及物体”和“断言物体存在”，发现现有幻觉检测器常把非断言性提及误判为幻觉。

Details

Motivation: 视觉语言模型物体幻觉通常通过检查描述中每个对象是否出现在图像里来评估，但句子提到对象并不总是在断言它存在，例如“供人坐的长椅”。把这种非断言性提及算作幻觉会夸大模型错误率。

Result: NAME-Bench从五种视觉语言模型为COCO图像生成的描述中收集25,497个物体提及，并标记为具体且存在、明确断言但不存在的幻觉、非断言性提及三类；非断言类再分为描绘、用途、否定和不确定表达四种。所有测试检测器都会把非断言提及误报为幻觉，比例为14%–80%。将非断言提及作为负例加入训练，可在LLaVA-1.5上大幅纠正问题；对四个较新的视觉语言模型，需要多5–10倍数据才能达到类似纠正，同时检测到的真实幻觉会减少。

Insight: 幻觉评估应判定文本是否断言物体存在，而不是仅凭词语提及来判断。结果表明新模型上的误报更难用额外负例纠正，且可能牺牲真实幻觉检出；摘要未给出每个检测器的具体误报率。

Abstract: Object hallucination in vision-language models (VLMs) is usually measured by checking each object a caption names against the image. Most benchmarks and detectors alike treat every object mention as a claim that the object is present. This assumption fails when a caption names an object without asserting it, as in “a bench for people to sit on”, which mentions people without claiming that any are in the image. We call such mentions non-assertive. Treating them as hallucinations inflates the error rates attributed to models that use such language. To separate them from real hallucinations, we introduce NAME-Bench (Non-Assertive Mention Evaluation Benchmark), 25,497 object mentions from captions that five VLMs generate for COCO images. Each mention is labelled concrete (asserted and present), hallucinated (asserted and absent), or non-assertive, and non-assertive mentions are further divided into four subtypes: depictive, purpose, negation and hedged. All the hallucination detectors that we evaluate flag non-assertive mentions as hallucinations, at rates between 14% and 80%. Adding non-assertive mentions to detector training as negatives largely corrects this on LLaVA-1.5. On the four newer VLMs, a comparable correction takes five to ten times more data, and the detectors then catch fewer real hallucinations. These findings show that current detectors conflate mentioning an object with asserting it, and that this conflation is harder to undo on the four newer VLMs than on LLaVA-1.5. Hallucination evaluation should therefore separate what a caption asserts from what it merely mentions.

[94] Diversity Without Labels: Task-Agnostic Coreset Selection Across Perception and Action cs.CVPDF

Julia Hindel, Benjamin Ramtoula, Kyuhwan Yeon, Nick Heppert, Paul Newman

TL;DR: CoreDNA以视觉基础模型神经元激活分布进行无监督、任务无关的核心集选择，在多种感知和行动任务上达到或超过现有方法。

Details

Motivation: 从大型数据集合中挑选高质量训练子集很困难。现有核心集方法常需代理模型训练、大量标注或针对具体任务设计；无监督特征方法则多依赖两两图像比较或固定子集对照。

Result: CoreDNA将视觉基础模型的神经元激活汇总成单图像和全数据集分布，通过比较单图像与数据集层级分布，同时捕捉低级视觉结构及高层语义。作者发现，高质量样本集中于最后一层激活统计的尾部，并通过与依赖训练的核心集方法对照验证；随后据此设计高效筛选机制，用于选择性标注和模型训练。在图像分类、语义分割、深度估计、端到端驾驶模仿学习及移动操作任务上，CoreDNA达到或超过先进方法。

Insight: 基于激活分布的简洁表征可跨任务筛选有价值样本，覆盖感知与行动任务。摘要没有给出各任务核心集比例、运行成本和绝对成绩，也未说明尾部样本识别在不同骨干上的稳定性。

Abstract: While data collection for model training is relatively easy, curating high-quality datasets from large collections is inherently challenging. Coreset estimation methods address this problem by identifying the best data subsets to train models on. However, existing approaches often require proxy model training, extensive annotations, or task-specific tailoring. Unsupervised alternatives typically exploit geometric properties in foundation model feature spaces, but are limited to pairwise image-to-image comparisons or comparisons against a fixed subset. We introduce CoreDNA, an unsupervised, task-agnostic coreset estimation approach that pools neuron activations from vision foundation models into per-image and per-dataset distributions. By comparing each image’s distribution to the dataset-level distribution, CoreDNA can capture both low-level visual structure and high-level semantic information across layers. We find that high-quality samples concentrate in the tails of the last layer’s activation statistics, which we validate by relating them to training-based coreset methods. Building on these findings, we develop a computationally efficient selection mechanism to construct task-agnostic, high-quality coresets for selective annotation and model training. Across image classification, semantic segmentation, depth estimation, and imitation learning for end-to-end driving and mobile manipulation, CoreDNA matches or outperforms the state of the art, demonstrating unprecedented generalization across perception and action tasks. These results highlight the effectiveness of compact activation-based representations for data selection.

Abstract: A 3D asset admits multiple valid part decompositions, while semantics-aware segmentation should reflect both the desired granularity and the parts a user wants to extract. Ideally, a practical segmentation model should strive for a simple principle: what you ask for is what you get. However, realizing this goal is hindered by two core obstacles: the lack of multi-granularity training data and the absence of a holistic control mechanism. We propose FLEXPART, a unified framework for text-controlled 3D mesh segmentation. To establish flexible control, we introduce a holistic control prompt comprising semantic part names with location and shape clauses. To support this mechanism, we utilize an automated Vision-Language Model (VLM) pipeline to construct a large-scale dataset of multi-granularity partitions and their corresponding prompts. Driven by this data, FLEXPART generates a view-independent part-identity field in native 3D space, directly assigning discrete part IDs to mesh faces. To compel the model to respect semantic conditions rather than rely on geometric priors, we train the model using randomized background dropping. Furthermore, an inference-time VLM planner can automatically complete descriptive clauses from 2D renderings, allowing users to simply name the desired parts in ordinary words. Abstract: Physical plausibility is essential for realistic video generation. Existing methods impose physical guidance before synthesis via simulations or refined prompts, but this can limit dynamic diversity, miss errors that emerge only after generation, and require repeated regeneration when inconsistencies remain. We therefore study a generate-diagnose-repair paradigm that preserves the original generation capability and selectively repairs physical inconsistencies afterward, raising two key questions: what to repair and how to repair? To address them, we introduce PhysRepair, an agentic video generation framework that combines causal reasoning over the observed physical failure with prior repair experience to enable localized and adaptive correction. Specifically, Spatiotemporal Causal Constraints identify the first invalid physical state and its causally affected states, producing a repair specification that guides physically plausible local regeneration while preserving unaffected content. Experience-augmented In-context Learning combines the ongoing repair context with relevant experience from previous repairs, allowing the agent to adapt its repair decisions across diverse physical failures and accumulate completed trajectories for future cases. Experiments on Physics-IQ Verified across solid mechanics, fluid dynamics, optics, thermodynamics, and magnetism demonstrate that PhysRepair consistently improves the physical plausibility of generated videos over existing methods. Our project page is available at https://xxkkxxx.github.io/PhysRepair-Website/.

Abstract: Infrared-visible image fusion (IVIF) aims to combine visible and thermal infrared observations for human perception and downstream vision tasks. Visible and thermal infrared observations are physically complementary but unreliable in different ways. Existing deep IVIF methods mainly improve feature interaction and reconstruction, but often lack an explicit local rule for deciding when visible appearance should dominate and when thermal responses should contribute. To address this issue, we reformulate IVIF as local Bayesian posterior rendering, where fusion is controlled by posterior action weights rather than direct modality mixing. In this paper, we propose Proposal-Context-Detail Fusion (PCDFusion), a visible-anchored framework that implements this formulation through three rendering stages. PCDFusion preserves potentially useful thermal responses, updates exposure-degraded visible regions, and renders reliable band-limited infrared structures into the final luminance. Extensive experiments on public IVIF benchmarks show that PCDFusion improves fusion quality, target recovery, and downstream detection and segmentation performance under challenging illumination. The code is available at https://github.com/BIT-RuiLiu/PCDFusion.git.

Abstract: Federated prompt learning enables vision-language models like CLIP to collaboratively adapt lightweight soft prompts across distributed clients without sharing raw data. However, existing federated prompt learning methods are designed primarily for supervised settings with labeled client data and assumed relatively stable training distributions. At test time, source-free federated prompt tuning remains largely unexplored. Clients may receive unlabeled target samples with heterogeneous, often non-IID distributions that differ substantially across clients and from the source training distribution, causing locally adapted prompts to drift in different directions and reducing the effectiveness of federated aggregation. In this work, we explore Source-Free Federated Test-Time Prompt Tuning (SF-FTTPT), where distributed clients collaboratively adapt soft prompts on unlabeled test streams without access to source data. Our study reveals that conventional federated aggregation methods struggle under heterogeneous client distributions, as locally adapted prompts become increasingly specialized and difficult to combine effectively. To address this challenge, we propose FedRAPP, a personalized aggregation framework that combines reliability-aware client assessment, server-side exponential moving-average prompt memory, and similarity-guided peer aggregation. FedRAPP selectively incorporates information from reliable and compatible clients while preserving local specialization, enabling more robust adaptation under distribution shift. Extensive experiments across nine CLIP benchmark datasets and ImageNet variants demonstrate that FedRAPP consistently outperforms standard federated aggregation baselines under heterogeneous client distributions while maintaining the efficiency advantages of prompt-only adaptation.

It is therefore necessary to examine both how much synthetic augmentation to use and which disorders benefit. We investigate disease-specific synthetic oversampling using images from the Phenotypic Disease Image Database to augment a subset of the GestaltMatcher Database. We compare increasing augmentation levels in an image-based model, GestaltMatcher-Arc, and a multimodal model, GestaltMML, while keeping the real training and patient-disjoint evaluation partitions fixed. Repeated training runs assess overall and disease-specific accuracy, and an exploratory embedding analysis examines changes in class compactness and separation. Result: Video Diffusion Avatars从单张图像创建可控头像，通过微调视频扩散模型控制相机视角、面部表情和上半身姿势；学习式表情空间可表达3DMM难以覆盖的舌头和口腔内部细节。作者在DISTRACT基准发现表情编码存在强视角偏差，并通过解耦消除，使其可用作多视角一致头像的驱动信号；相机和上身姿势则通过代理网格渲染联合控制。相机三元组训练策略仅用公开多视角数据，在单张A100上训练34小时。在BecomingLit新视角重演中，相比先进单图3D头像，面部LPIPS降低29%，JOD感知质量提高66%；静态3D高斯重建探针也显示多视角一致性最高。生成结果还呈现显式渲染器较难实现的头发动态、阴影、反射和折射。

Insight: 视频生成先验可扩展单图3D头像在外观和动态上的表现，但需要处理表情码中的视角偏差。摘要未提供完整控制精度、身份保持数值及推理延迟；34小时是特定单A100训练配置。

Abstract: Video diffusion models (VDMs) trained on internet-scale data encode a rich prior on appearance, motion, and physics that is difficult to replicate with explicit 3D rendering and costly 3D data capture. We introduce Video Diffusion Avatars (VDAs), a method that creates photo-realistic, multi-view consistent avatars from a single image by fine-tuning a VDM for precise control over camera viewpoint, facial expression, and upper-body pose. Departing from 3D morphable models (3DMMs), VDAs use a learned expression space that lifts the expressiveness ceiling of 3DMMs, e.g., for the tongue and mouth interior. However, learned expression codes exhibit heavy viewpoint bias, as our DISTRACT benchmark reveals. We show that this bias can be removed through disentanglement, enabling their use as driving signals for multi-view consistent avatars. Camera and upper-body pose are controlled jointly via proxy-mesh renderings, giving independent control over expression, pose, and viewpoint. A camera-triplet training strategy makes the approach efficient: our models train for 34 hours on a single A100 using only public multi-view data. VDAs outperform state-of-the-art single-image 3D avatars on novel-view reenactment, reducing face LPIPS by 29% and improving perceptual quality (JOD) by 66% on BecomingLit. A static 3DGS reconstruction probe further confirms that our generations are the most multi-view consistent. Built on a video prior, VDAs produce effects beyond the reach of explicit renderers, including physically plausible hair dynamics, shadows, reflections, and refractions.

[104] Persistent Planar Memory for Video World Models cs.CVPDF

Yuze He, Bin Tan, Zelin Gao, Yujun Shen, Yong-Jin Liu

TL;DR: PPMem用紧凑平面基元作为视频世界模型的持久三维记忆，每个自回归片段联合生成视频与平面几何，并在后续生成中通过渲染深度复用场景结构。

Motivation: 大规模生成和探索环境的视频世界模型需要长期保存已生成内容的三维结构。像素级点云记忆冗余且成本高，而室内外场景的墙面、地面和立面等主要结构可由少量平面表示。

Result: PPMem在视频扩散Transformer中加入平面预测头和深度token融合模块，每个自回归片段同时生成RGB视频和平面几何，合并成持久平面记忆；后续片段以渲染深度为条件使用该记忆。作者报告单个场景约10万平面基元、占5MB，比逐像素点云记忆紧凑约三个数量级，同时提供度量深度、法线和可导出表面。共同骨干预测使其跨视角一致，合并过程可随生成进行细化。在DL3DV和RealEstate10K上，PPMem的视频质量超过先前三维约束世界模型，并能在数百帧及大视角变化下保持连贯，同时生成度量尺度深度、表面法线和平面几何。

Abstract: Video world models that generate and explore environments at scale need a persistent 3D memory of what they have produced. Plane primitives offer a natural candidate: a compact set of oriented planes captures the dominant structural elements (e.g., walls, floors, facades) of indoor and outdoor scenes, while requiring only 7 parameters per primitive. We present PPMem, a video world model that adopts plane primitives as its persistent 3D memory. PPMem augments a video diffusion transformer with a planar prediction head and a Depth Token Fusion (DTF) module: each autoregressive chunk jointly produces RGB video and planar geometry, which merges into a persistent plane memory that conditions subsequent chunks via rendered depth. The resulting memory is three orders of magnitude more compact than per-pixel point clouds (~100K primitives, 5MB for a full scene), geometrically explicit (metric depth, normals, exportable surfaces), cross-view consistent (jointly predicted by the shared backbone), and adaptive (refined by the merging procedure as generation proceeds). On DL3DV and RealEstate10K, PPMem outperforms prior 3D-grounded world models in video quality while jointly producing metric-scale depth, surface normals, and exportable planar geometry, sustaining coherence across hundreds of frames under large viewpoint changes. Project page: https://hyzcluster.github.io/ppmem.

TL;DR: StopWatch发现物体目标导航策略的主要失误可能在目标定位而非探索：模型常未发现目标已在视野中，也难估计距离；显式目标定位提升多个基准表现。

Details

Motivation: 预训练视觉语言模型带来丰富语义知识，微调为导航策略已很常见，但成功率仍低，且策略具体欠缺何种能力并不明确。

Result: 作者诊断发现，既有策略通常能进行探索，却会漏看已经出现在视野里的目标，或不能估计目标距离，说明目标落地定位是主要失败来源。显式对目标进行定位后，成功率显著提高，并在三个场景集合的六个基准上达到先进结果。研究还修正了这些基准中的标注缺陷，使后续评测更可靠。在清理后的基准上提供真实定位结果时，策略表现几乎追平生成示范的专家，表明剩余空间主要在感知而非策略学习。

Abstract: Pretrained VLMs bring rich world knowledge and semantic priors to object-goal navigation, and fine-tuning a VLM into a navigation policy has become a common recipe. Despite this progress, success rates remain low, and it is unclear which capability these fine-tuned policies lack. Our diagnosis identifies goal grounding as the dominant failure. These policies explore well, yet often fail to notice that the goal is already in view or to estimate how far away it is. To address this failure, we ground the goal explicitly. Explicit grounding substantially improves success and reaches state-of-the-art results on six benchmarks across three scene collections. We also correct annotation defects in these benchmarks, so that future methods are evaluated more faithfully. Our analysis on the clean benchmarks shows that, with ground-truth grounding, our policy nearly matches the expert that generated its demonstrations, leaving the headroom in perception rather than policy learning. Our work points to goal grounding as a missing piece of object-goal navigation and offers a simple and effective solution to the task.

[106] Rethinking the design space of Vanilla Pixel Diffusion Transformers cs.CV | cs.AIPDF

Soumik Mukhopadhyay, Long Mai, Abhinav Shrivastava, Aniruddha Mahapatra

TL;DR: MFlow为像素空间扩散Transformer学习在干净图像预测与速度预测间按数据主成分方向自适应插值，并用后处理卷积模块补偿生成图像的高频细节。

Details

Motivation: 像素空间扩散可避免潜空间压缩损失和单独训练自编码器；近期研究显示普通扩散Transformer可直接预测干净图像，但预测参数化是否应固定尚未充分研究。

Result: MFlow在数据主成分方向间学习干净图像x0预测与速度u预测的端到端插值。作者发现偏好的参数化随数据表示和模型容量系统性变化，模型容量增大时更偏向速度预测。在ImageNet、UCF-101视频和大规模文生图任务上，MFlow实现有竞争力或更好的生成表现。研究还发现，无论参数化如何，普通像素扩散Transformer相对真实图像仍缺少高频成分；为此提出ConvRefiner，在冻结扩散骨干的条件下用对抗损失进行少量后训练，可恢复生成图像的高频统计，同时保持FID。

Abstract: Pixel-space diffusion models have recently re-emerged as a compelling alternative to latent diffusion, avoiding the lossy latent bottleneck and separately trained autoencoder. JiT shows that a vanilla diffusion transformer can generate directly in pixel space by replacing the commonly used velocity prediction ($\boldsymbol{u}$-prediction) with clean image prediction ($\boldsymbol{x_0}$-prediction). In this work, we revisit this choice and introduce MFlow, an adaptive parameterization that learns to interpolate between $\boldsymbol{x_0}$- and $\boldsymbol{u}$-prediction across principal directions of the data, learned end-to-end with diffusion training. We find that the preferred parameterization varies systematically with the data representation and model capacity – notably, increasing model capacity shifts preference toward velocity prediction. We evaluate MFlow across ImageNet, UCF-101 video, and large-scale text-to-image generation, demonstrating competitive or improved generative performance across diverse settings. In addition, we find that vanilla pixel diffusion transformers, irrespective of parameterization, retain a persistent high-frequency deficit relative to real images. To address this, we introduce ConvRefiner, a lightweight convolutional module trained post-hoc with an adversarial loss while keeping the diffusion backbone frozen. With only a small amount of additional training, ConvRefiner restores the high-frequency statistics of generated images while preserving FID. Project webpage: https://soumik-kanad.github.io/mflow-diffusion/.

Abstract: Generated videos often lose geometric consistency under camera motion: structures warp under viewpoint change, parallax becomes inconsistent, and smooth camera movement may collapse into shot-like transitions. We argue that this failure is largely a data problem. In web-scale video corpora, most visible motion comes from dynamic objects captured by static or weakly moving cameras, leaving only sparse supervision for viewpoint-induced appearance changes. To address this, we propose Video Geometry Corrector (VGC), a post-hoc video-to-video model trained on synthetic paired supervision. Starting from real videos with diverse camera motion and pose annotations, we construct VG-Pairs with a synthetic paired-supervision data generation pipeline. Each video clip is treated as a geometry-consistent target and paired with a distorted counterpart synthesized by a pretrained image-to-video inpainting model using trajectory-adaptive keyframes. VGC learns to map the distorted input to the geometry-consistent target without modifying the source generator, and uses optical flow as an auxiliary cue to help disentangle camera motion from object motion. Experiments on pose-conditioned and prompt-driven benchmarks show that VGC improves geometric consistency across multiple source generators, and scaling to a larger backbone further extends the framework toward more general video refinement.

Details

Motivation: 词语含义理论上与它的字体或装饰风格无关，但视觉语言模型可能把文字图像中的可见风格当作概念语义线索，影响属性描述。需要区分模型是否看懂词语和是否受呈现样式干扰。

Result: 实验比较强调可读性的功能性文字风格（如黑色无衬线字）与强调展示效果的装饰风格（如彩色手写体）。研究只考察视觉语言模型能够正确识别图像中文字所指概念的情形，此时文字样式不应改变其对概念属性的描述。结果显示，即使概念识别正确，文字呈现风格仍会影响模型生成的属性描述，体现视觉样式向语义推断的非平凡泄漏。

Abstract: When the visual style of text is considered, a wide variety can be observed in font, color, and size. In this paper, we investigate whether, and how, the style in which a word is visualized in an image impacts the description that a Large Visual Language Model (LVLM) provides for the concept to which that word refers. Specifically, we investigate how functional text styles (readability-oriented, e.g., black sans-serif) versus decorative styles (display-oriented, e.g., colored cursive/script) affect LVLMs’ descriptions of a concept in terms of the attributes of that concept. Our experiments study the situation in which the LVLM is able to correctly identify the concept referred to by a visual text, i.e., by a word or words rendered as an image, and in which the visual text style should not influence the attribute-based description that the LVLM produces. Our experimental results reveal that even when the concept is correctly identified, text style influences the model’s attribute-based descriptions of the concept. Our findings demonstrate non-trivial style leakage from text style into semantic inference and motivate style-aware evaluation and mitigation for LVLM-based multimedia systems.

[109] PRIME: Towards Verifiable Evidence-Grounded Reasoning in LLM Agents cs.AI | cs.CLPDF

Hamed Khosravi, Xiaoming Huo

TL;DR: PRIME在证据检查前预先固定允许支持结论的证据路径，并由独立程序记录检查结果、拒绝无依据结论，提升组织规则核查的可验证性。

Details

Motivation: 智能体核对漏洞报告等记录时，要求可能允许多种满足方式；模型可能在某项必查结果失败后仍接受报告，也可能未尝试另一条可行路径就给出较弱结论。仅由模型文本推理难以确保结论受证据支持。

Result: PRIME先制定每种结论可接受的证据路线，再由独立程序登记每项检查结果并阻止证据未授权的结论；若仍有检查可能支持更强结论，系统会提示智能体继续检查或自行执行。只报告规则与证据台账授权的结论。在480个公开记录任务上，直接向智能体展示要求时，26.7%的任务出现无依据结论；PRIME未报告任何无依据结论。指引和补充检查将可证实结论的平均分从仅拒绝时的30.1提升至59.2满分中的最高值。作者指出，在要求正确、检查位置已知且预算足够的条件下，完成流程可达到证据所支持的最强结论。

Insight: 把证据规则与检查台账从语言模型判断中分离，能明确哪些结论被授权，并提示如何补足证据。零无依据结论依赖正确要求、已知检查位置和充分预算等假设；摘要未报告程序错误或开放式证据发现情境。

Abstract: LLM agents are used to check records against an organization’s requirements, for example whether a vulnerability report is ready for investigation. Requirements often allow several ways to be met, such as a severity rating with a curator-reviewed advisory or a published advisory link. Agents fail by accepting a report after a required check has failed, or by settling for a weaker conclusion without trying another way that could still succeed. We introduce PRIME (Precommitted Rules for Inference from Minimal Evidence). Before checking, it fixes evidence routes specifying how each conclusion may be justified. A separate program records each check result and refuses any conclusion the results do not justify. It also points the agent to checks that could justify a stronger conclusion, or runs them itself. PRIME reports only conclusions authorized by its rules and ledger. With correct requirements, exact checks at known locations, and sufficient budget at stopping, completion reaches the strongest supported conclusion. On 480 public-record tasks, an agent shown the requirements reported unjustified conclusions on 26.7% of tasks, while PRIME reported none. Its directions and added checks raised the average score for justified conclusions from 30.1, with refusal alone, to the maximum of 59.2 out of 100.

Abstract: Long-horizon GUI agents rely on visual history to maintain task states, but retaining historical screenshots increases visual-token usage and inference costs, while textual summaries can discard critical state information, undermining long-horizon reliability. We propose ECHOES, which leverages training-time privileged information to guide textual history compression and strengthen long-horizon reasoning. Specifically, ECHOES adopts a Compression-Think-Action (CTA) format to recursively update a textual history state and reason. The same model, given privileged multi-image inputs, serves as a teacher that provides dense token-level on-policy distillation on student-generated CTA rollouts. Our Perception-Memory Fidelity (PMF) Gap-guided OPD-RL framework dynamically balances privileged distillation and task-reward optimization, with periodic teacher updates supporting continued improvement. We further introduce ECHOESBMK, a fine-grained diagnostic benchmark derived from real GUI agent failure trajectories to jointly evaluate visual perception and cross-modal state retention. Experiments on four GUI benchmarks and ECHOESBMK demonstrate substantial gains in task performance and memory fidelity. Without retaining historical screenshots at deployment, ECHOES achieves competitive performance with a favorable performance-inference-cost trade-off and exhibits positive zero-shot transfer to unseen visual planning tasks.

Details

Motivation: 大型推理模型通过测试时计算在数学和科学基准上成绩强，但日常推理常依赖语境和语用判断，不完全符合教科书式形式逻辑，其能力尚未被充分评估。

Abstract: Large reasoning models (LRMs) that leverage test-time computation achieve strong performance on mathematical and scientific benchmarks and are increasingly deployed in real-world applications. However, their ability to perform \textit{everyday logical reasoning}, which often departs from textbook formal logic, remains underexplored. In this work, we systematically evaluate how well reasoning models align with everyday reasoning patterns by introducing a benchmark based on linguistic and pragmatic reasoning phenomena. We also compare reasoning models with non-reasoning models under controlled prompting conditions. We find that, in many cases, (1) reasoning models show degraded performance in everyday reasoning when such reasoning deviates from formal logical rules, (2) some non-reasoning models with chain-of-thought prompting outperform comparable reasoning models in everyday reasoning, and (3) prompting with additional instructions provides limited control over reasoning behavior. These findings suggest that current reasoning models do not reliably capture everyday reasoning and point to limitations in post-training, motivating improved training objectives and evaluation across diverse forms of reasoning.

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as formalizers: given a reasoning problem expressed in natural language, they produce a formal specification that a symbolic solver can then solve, with guarantees relative to that specification. Many specifications are runnable yet semantically wrong, encoding incorrect constraints, domains, objectives, predicates, or goals, and solver-error feedback dries up once execution succeeds. Purely LLM-based diagnosis, lacking a formal ground truth, often fails to spot these errors, and its ungrounded critiques cause regressions in 11.8% of initially correct specifications in our experiments. We propose DRAGO, a repair framework that replaces this ungrounded critique with deterministic, formalism-specific diagnosis, and retains a rewrite only when the diagnosed error weight strictly decreases under a reference-free acceptance predicate. Across five benchmarks spanning logical reasoning (SMT-LIB2), constraint programming (MiniZinc), and planning (PDDL), DRAGO cuts regressions by nearly an order of magnitude, to 1.3%, while improving answer accuracy by 12.9 percentage points over zero-shot formalization and 8.7 percentage points over LLM-diagnostic repair, averaged across benchmarks. Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable success in complex reasoning through explicit Chain-of-Thought (CoT), but the associated computational overhead and token verbosity remain significant barriers to efficiency. While implicit reasoning in continuous latent spaces offers a promising alternative, existing methods like Coconut and CODI often rely on fixed-length latent sequences and suffer from representational collapse. In this paper, we propose Sep-Latent, an adaptive implicit reasoning framework that utilizes separator tokens as structural semantic anchors. Abstract: A key challenge in on-policy distillation (OPD) is extracting effective supervision from teacher knowledge. Supervision from token probabilities may underutilize internal knowledge, while direct hidden-state matching forces the student to shift to the teacher’s feature space and complicates cross-architecture transfer. Inspired by contrastive learning’s use of relationships between instances, we propose \textbf{SimOPD} to transfer teacher internal knowledge through response similarities. Specifically, SimOPD uses paired memory banks of student-generated responses to extract supervision from the teacher’s internal representations as similarity distributions. Details

Motivation: 链式思维可控性影响推理模型的实用性和监督可靠性。既有研究只测模型是否服从用户提示中的思维链约束，并报告各模型可控性较低；作者认为这一结果可能源于约束表达位置不合适。

Result: Self-Instructed CoT Controllability把约束写在模型自身思维链内，作为对用户提示评测的补充。实验显示，自我指令下模型可控性显著高于标准用户指令；即使只提醒模型遵守用户约束，也能大幅改善。有效自我指令还可通过用户提示中的简单说明引导生成，使模型无需访问推理通道也能提高控制。作者进一步在真实监控设置中展示，自我指令能够用于规避思维链监控。

Insight: 较低的CoT可控性可能受约束提示位置影响，但提升可控性同时带来监控规避风险。摘要没有给出具体可控性数值、模型范围和监控攻击成功率，因此安全结论限于所示设置。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) controllability, the ability of reasoning models to control properties of their CoT, is consequential for both their usefulness and reliable oversight. Recent work evaluates this capability by measuring whether models follow CoT constraints given in the user prompt, and reports very low controllability across models. We argue that this low controllability may not reflect an inherent inability of reasoning models to control their CoT, but instead result from naively specifying constraints in the user prompt. To test this, we introduce Self-Instructed CoT Controllability: a complementary evaluation that places the constraint as a self-instruction inside the model’s CoT and measures its ability to follow it. We show that, under self-instruction, models achieve significantly higher controllability than with standard user instructions. Even a generic reminder to follow the user’s constraint substantially improves controllability. Moreover, effective self-instructions can be bootstrapped into the reasoning through a simple instruction in the user prompt, enabling improved control even without access to the reasoning channel. Finally, using self-instruction, we demonstrate in a realistic monitoring setup that CoT controllability can be leveraged for monitor evasion, posing a risk to CoT monitorability.

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate fluent outputs that lack source support or conflict with local operational constraints. We introduce Self-Reflective Chain-of-Thought (SRCoT), a modular architecture that organizes generation as a closed Retrieve–Reflect–Revise loop. SRCoT separates environment state, persistent knowledge, accumulated experience, and short-term memory into provenance-labeled stores. BM25 retrieval supplies local context; subsequent passes expose intermediate drafts, missing-information markers, follow-up queries, and optional claim–evidence mappings. These artifacts inform retrieval, revision, clarification, or abstention decisions. We examine the architecture through qualitative, trace-based case studies in two fully synthetic settings: emergency medical services and Munich-Sim, an emergency-operations simulation. Abstract: Generative models now synthesise video that is hard to distinguish from genuine footage, and learned detectors that key on a generator’s artefacts degrade sharply on unseen models. A unified two-channel detector extracts the ENF from audio and from rolling-shutter video through one second harmonic estimator and a shared network; a synthetic-injection training method, driven by an Ornstein-Uhlenbeck drift model and anchored to real recordings, overcomes the scarcity of labelled ENF data; a reference-matching channel verifies a claimed time of recording and recovers an unknown one; and a two-axis agentic orchestrator fuses recording authenticity with content-manipulation evidence under an asymmetric rule, escalating expensive detectors only when needed. Abstract: Recent advances in video generation have increased the demand for reliable watermarking for copyright protection, yet the robustness of video watermarks against removal attacks remains underexplored. Most watermark-removal methods originate from the image domain: signal optimization injects imperceptible pixel-domain perturbations, whereas generative resynthesis produces a visually similar instance through diffusion synthesis. When extended to videos, the former introduces temporally varying noise that causes visible flickering, while the latter regenerates fine-grained structures inconsistently across frames, leading to structural motion inconsistency. To evaluate video watermark robustness, we propose PoseRegen, a pose-consistent reconstruction framework that removes watermarks while preserving the original content and motion. PoseRegen first applies a temporally shared viewpoint transformation to disrupt watermark-bearing spatial patterns and then jointly reconstructs the transformed sequence with a pose-conditioned video model. A frozen video DiT processes the complete sequence with its reference-video latent, and full-sequence Flow Matching supervision keeps identity and background consistent across frames. To further preserve motion structure, we introduce a lightweight Pose-Conditioned Motion Consistency Adapter that adapts the frozen backbone to temporally ordered pose guidance and constrains body-part trajectories throughout reconstruction. Experiments on eleven video watermarking schemes show that PoseRegen reduces the average bit accuracy to 0.505, near random guessing, while outperforming the strongest baseline by 9.34% on motion-related VBench metrics. These results reveal a practical vulnerability of current video watermarks and highlight the need to evaluate robustness jointly with temporal and motion quality.

Motivation: 智能体的长期记忆可能从外部来源吸收未经授权的主张，之后却把它们当成用户偏好、政策或权限使用。仅按内容过滤或粗粒度追踪来源，既可能漏掉越权影响，也可能阻断有用信息。

Result: SAFE-Mem为每条主张标注类别、权限来源和适用范围三种治理标签；检索时将问题要求与主张许可匹配，以阻断未经授权的信息影响并保留合法记忆。治理记忆图连接源句、主张和实体，支持溯源及高效检索。在四种LLM骨干、MPBench和LoCoMo上的实验中，相较仍保留合法记忆使用能力的防御方法，SAFE-Mem攻击成功率最低，至少比其中最强者低77%；在所有被评防御中回答准确率最高，最多提高15%。

Insight: 记忆权限需要保留细粒度来源和使用范围，才能同时考虑安全与效用。摘要未给出攻击类型分项、标签生成方式及人工标注成本，因此效果依赖治理元数据是否正确。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly rely on persistent memory to support long-term reasoning and personalized interactions. However, information collected from external sources can introduce security risks when untrusted claims (i.e., atomic statements) are later treated as authorized preferences, policies, or permissions. Existing defenses typically rely on content-based filtering or coarse-grained provenance tracking, which either fail to prevent unauthorized memory use or block legitimate information, sacrificing memory utility. To address these limitations, we propose SAFE-Mem, a fine-grained memory governance framework that improves security while preserving legitimate memory use. First, SAFE-Mem annotates each claim with three governance labels (i.e., kind, authority, and scope) to capture its provenance and permitted use. Second, governance-aware retrieval matches query requirements with claim permissions to block unauthorized influence while retaining legitimate information. Third, a governance-enabled memory graph connects source sentences, claims, and entities to support provenance tracking and efficient retrieval. Across four LLM backbones on MPBench and LoCoMo, SAFE-Mem attains the lowest attack success among defenses that retain legitimate memory, at least 77% below the strongest of them, and the highest answer accuracy of all evaluated defenses, improving it by up to 15%. The code is available at https://github.com/LVenum/SAFE-Mem.

Abstract: Reasoning rerankers write a rationale before ranking a candidate slate, and existing systems read item metadata to do so, even though generative recommenders increasingly represent items as compact Semantic IDs (SIDs). Ranking from SIDs alone has two appeals: SID prompts are far shorter than the matched item text, and although item codes are fixed from content, the backbone learns their token embeddings in part from user-item interaction sequences, so the tokens can carry behavioral signal that item text lacks. That training targets next-item generation, and whether it supports ranking a supplied slate from SIDs alone is untested. We build ReRankID, a controlled testbed in which models reason and rank from a history and slate shown only as SIDs, varying item rendering, history length, and slate size one at a time. On this testbed, such models often copy the input order instead of reranking, and readable metadata does not repair the failure. We compare supervised fine-tuning, rejection-sampled self-training, on-policy distillation, and verifier-based reinforcement learning as post-training methods and find that they elicit the missing behavior. The strongest post-trained model, the 8B model trained with a graded ranking reward, gives the best SID-only ranking and matches strong text-reading LLMs whose prompts are about 20x longer. It ranks above random with no history at all, where a caption-reading reference does not, and it keeps its gains at long histories, where text prompts pass 50k tokens and the caption-reading reference stops improving. In a paired rationale audit, this model argues trade-offs better than that reference but describes items less accurately. The common post-training methods we test thus elicit ranking from SIDs but not faithful item descriptions, which calls for post-training that improves the reasoning itself, not only the ranking it produces.

Abstract: Automated fundus-based diabetic retinopathy (DR) grading can substantially expand screening capacity, but source-trained models degrade sharply across different cameras, clinics, and patient populations. Existing test-time adaptation methods depend on entropy minimization or target information accumulated from image batches and streams. These mechanisms can reinforce confidently incorrect predictions and break down when clinical images arrive independently. We introduce CF-NODE, a novel counterfactual test-time adaptation framework for cross- domain DR grading. Instead of trusting prediction confidence or estimating a target distribution, CF-NODE asks how each prediction responds under a controlled intervention on the evidence supporting it. CF-NODE ranks internal spatial nodes using model-derived attention, feature magnitude, and node-set similarity, progressively attenuates the highest-ranked nodes, and uses the stability of the resulting expected-grade response to apply a bounded calibration of the predicted grade distribution. The framework is fully frozen and adapts every image independently using only geometric views and a fixed source-derived prior, requiring no target-derived information or model updates. We evaluate CF-NODE on five external DR datasets under repeated-seed evaluation against TENT, EATA, SAR, and T3A. CF-NODE outperforms all four methods with statistically significant improvements over the second-best method, improves AUC on every target domain, and attains the highest average AUC and macro-F1, delivering stronger grade discrimination together with more balanced recognition across DR severity levels. These results demonstrate that counterfactual intervention sensitivity provides consistent and deployment-ready domain robustness for single-image clinical inference.

Motivation: 大语言模型已可提出、筛选和执行机器学习实验，若要自动改进模型，需要在投入漫长训练之前判断实验是否值得运行。但当前LLM对自拟实验的效果预测准确度尚缺乏实证衡量。

Abstract: Recent advances in AI make Recursive Self-Improvement (RSI) increasingly plausible. Large Language Models (LLMs) can already propose, select, and run Machine Learning (ML) experiments. However, the space of possible design choices in ML is vast. An LLM that proposes an experiment needs a reliable intuition of whether it is worth running given the timing constraints of long training runs. In this work, we measure such intuition in current frontier LLMs by evaluating how accurately they predict the impact of self-generated ML experiment ideas before these are put into training runs. To this end, we construct a dataset of 288 LLM-proposed ideas on eight MLE-bench tasks. We then prompt five frontier LLMs (GPT-6 Astra, Claude Opus 5.5, Claude Fable 5.1, Kimi K3, and GLM-5.3) to predict the impact of each idea and compare their predictions with the actual outcomes after training. We report four main findings. First, LLM predictions are substantially better than random chance but far from accurate: even the best model, Claude Opus 5.5, explains only half of the variance in outcomes (R^2 = 0.49). Second, we notice that all tested models systematically underestimate the magnitude of the changes. Third, prediction quality varies widely across tasks. Finally, the choice of the coding harness that actually implements the ideas has little effect on outcomes, and we find no evidence of self-preference. Higher reasoning settings and additional context lead to modest gains. Overall, our results show that current LLMs have useful but limited intuition about which ML ideas will work. Bangji Yang, Hongbo Ma, Ruihan Guo, Jiajun Fan, Xi Zhu
