Abstract: Long rollout windows make reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) expensive, yet simply shortening them can remove the correctness signal: when every single-problem rollout truncates before an answer, group-relative accuracy advantages vanish. As a technical continuation of Batched Contextual Reinforcement (BCR), we study how concurrent rewards relax this dependence on long windows. Concurrent Constraint Reinforcement (CCR) places three independently verifiable problems in one capped training completion. Correct answers completed before truncation retain credit, while a shared budget encourages shorter useful reasoning without an explicit length penalty. We connect this mechanism to conditional reward- and update-variance analyses and study 4B and 8B mathematical reasoning models. Against single-problem GRPO with a 32k window, CCR with an 8,000-token window reduces tokens per training step from 975,464 to 174,957 on 4B and from 793,832 to 165,788 on 8B, saving 82.1% and 79.1%, respectively. An 8B training trace shows increasing accuracy reward alongside shorter completions. Single-problem evaluations provide supporting evidence of retained reasoning quality. These results identify reward-preserving training structure as a practical route to lower per-step cost in short-window RLVR.

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) have emerged as a promising alternative to autoregressive language models, enabling non-autoregressive text generation through parallel decoding. Existing dLLMs can be broadly categorized into two families: masked diffusion models (MDMs) and uniform diffusion models (UDMs) In this work, we theoretically show that MDMs are easier to optimize due to their mask-based transition schedule but have lower computational expressivity, whereas UDMs enable iterative state revision and greater expressivity but are substantially harder to train. This trade-off raises a natural question: can we obtain the expressivity of iterative revision without giving up the simple, easy-to-optimize transition schedule of MDMs? Motivated by this analysis, we propose Lookahead Editing (\ours{}), which augments masked diffusion decoding with a rewritable proposal state. At each decoding step, the model performs several lookahead updates to iteratively refine future predictions in this proposal state before committing tokens to the masked sequence. This design introduces a rewritable proposal that uses additional compute to improve expressivity while keeping the simple mask-based transition schedule of MDMs. We fine-tune Qwen2.5-7B and Qwen3-8B to obtain LED-\texttt{7B} and LED-\texttt{8B}, respectively. Across eight benchmarks, LED SFT yields average gains of $+11.4$ and $+7.7$ points over standard MDM SFT. Moreover, using SFT alone, LED-\texttt{8B} outperforms LLaDA2.1-16B Q-mode, which is trained with continued pre-training, supervised fine-tuning, and reinforcement learning.

TL;DR: TreeOPSD把长时程智能体交互构成树状分支，通过终端奖励回传隐式回合信用，并将其转成有界教师分布进行后验自蒸馏。

Details

Motivation: 长时程强化学习只用终端奖励会导致回合级信用稀疏且方差高；固定rollout批次上顺序更新又会使当前策略偏离生成样本的行为策略，产生策略陈旧和更新过冲。既有方法需额外价值网络、步骤标注或更强外部教师。

Result: TreeOPSD将每轮交互视为树节点，从共享前缀分叉探索不同后续，并递归将终端奖励回传为隐式回合信用。每个信用被映射到由相对冻结行为策略似然比参数化的闭式有界教师分布；学生最小化与该教师分布的差异，在行为策略处切向恢复策略梯度方向，同时将顺序小批次更新约束到有限目标。多个基准和模型规模上的实验显示智能体能力提高，长时程任务尤为明显；例如Qwen3-30B-A3B-Thinking-2507在Deep Research上的平均准确率比GRPO高4.97分。

Insight: 树状分支可从共享状态构造更密集的过程信用，有界教师目标则针对固定rollout顺序更新过冲。摘要未列出其余基准结果、额外分支采样开销和与其他信用分配方法的全面比较。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards has become a scalable post-training paradigm for large language models. However, training agents over long-horizon interactions presents two challenges: using only terminal rewards makes turn-level credit assignment sparse and high-variance, while sequential mini-batch updates on a fixed rollout batch introduce policy staleness as the current policy drifts from the behavior policy. Existing approaches obtain denser credit via learned critics, process reward models, or on-policy distillation, but they rely on a value network, step-level annotations, or a stronger external teacher that increase training complexity. Meanwhile, importance sampling and clipping mitigate policy drift, but they do not specify a finite target that stops sequential updates from overshooting. In this work, we introduce TreeOPSD, a critic-free, tree-aware posterior self-distillation framework for long-horizon agentic reinforcement learning. Treating each interaction turn as a tree node, TreeOPSD branches alternative continuations from shared prefixes and recursively backs up terminal rewards into implicit turn-level credit. It then maps each credit to a closed-form, bounded teacher distribution parameterized by the likelihood ratio relative to the frozen behavior policy. Minimizing the divergence between this teacher and the student policy recovers the policy-gradient direction as the tangent at the behavior policy while bounding updates toward a finite target across sequential mini-batches. Empirically, TreeOPSD improves agentic capabilities across several competitive benchmarks and model scales, especially for long-horizon tasks, e.g., raising the average accuracy of Qwen3-30B-A3B-Thinking-2507 by 4.97 points over GRPO on the Deep Research setting.

[129] Has GPT-6-Astra Achieved Embodied Intelligence? cs.RO | cs.CV | cs.LG | cs.MMPDF

SenseNova, Joint Research Team, :, Zhongang Cai, Ziang Cao

TL;DR: 该报告在12个代表性基准和8种真实机器人配置上评估GPT-6-Astra具身能力，认为其展现广泛操作与导航能力，但距离实用具身智能仍有部署差距。

Details

Motivation: 通用多模态大模型的机器人操作和导航能力已挑战单任务、单具身专用模型范式，但基准中的提示学习表现能否迁移到真实机器人，以及高层推理和低层控制如何配合，仍需系统分析。

Result: 作者在桌面操作、人形机器人、移动平台和灵巧手共12个基准上，用训练示范进行上下文学习并显著增加执行步数，报告GPT-6-Astra取得此前未有的性能。研究指出，GPT-6-Astra作为高层System 2推理器与π0.5等低层System 1控制器闭环协调成本较高但有效。8种机器人配置的真实部署揭示示范采集昂贵，且受物理约束限制，不能简单通过延长执行时间换取更高成功率。作者还提出一种基准原型：任务来自现实需求、不提供域内示范，并用在智能体思考期间持续运行的世界时钟评估。

Insight: 报告将基准能力与真实机器人实用性区分，结论认为GPT-6-Astra基本达到具身能力门槛，但尚未达到实用具身智能。摘要未给出12个基准及8种机器人上的具体成绩，且结果依赖示范、执行步数和控制器协同方式。

Abstract: GPT-6-Astra demonstrates a level of competence in robotic manipulation and navigation that challenges the conventional paradigm of building specialized models for individual tasks and embodiments. In this report, we ask not only how capable GPT-6-Astra is, but what its capabilities imply for the field. First, we probe the limits of standalone GPT-6-Astra. We conduct a large-scale evaluation across 12 representative benchmarks spanning tabletop manipulation, humanoids, mobile platforms, and dexterous hands. Under a unified protocol that allows in-context learning (ICL) from training-set demonstrations and substantially increases the number of execution steps, GPT-6-Astra achieves unprecedented performance (Fig. 1). Future research may focus on improving 1) the generality of ICL 2) cost and execution time, 3) physical-state verification, 4) recovery from irreversible failures, and 5) long-horizon preservation of progress. Second, we study the agentic harness for embodied control. We identify that close-loop coordination between a high-level “System 2” reasoner (i.e., GPT-6-Astra) and a low-level “System 1” controller (e.g., π0.5) is costly but effective. Third, we examine GPT-6-Astra on real-world deployment. Our experiments on 8 robot configurations reveal substantial gaps: 1) robot demonstrations required for ICL are expensive to collect, and 2) it is implausible to trade longer execution for higher success rates with physical constraints. Fourth, we envision the next generation of embodied benchmarks. We develop a prototype that 1) derives tasks from real-world needs, 2) provides no in-domain demonstrations, and 3) judges agents on a world clock that keeps running while they deliberate. Taken together, our results suggest that GPT-6-Astra has largely crossed the threshold of embodied competence, but not yet that of practical embodied intelligence.

[130] MambaAct: Efficient Mamba Action Experts for Flow-Matching Vision-Language-Action Models cs.RO | cs.CVPDF

Khuyen Pham, Tran Nguyen Le, Edoardo Fazzari, Cesare Stefanini

TL;DR: MambaAct用紧凑Mamba模块替代流匹配视觉语言动作模型中的Transformer动作专家，将动作专家缩小10倍并降低推理延迟。

Details

Motivation: 视觉语言动作模型在机器人操作任务上潜力强，但流匹配策略需要在迭代去噪中反复调用动作专家，造成实时控制推理成本过高。

Result: MambaAct保留π0.5的预训练视觉语言前缀编码器，以Mamba动作专家替换Transformer动作专家，由其建模连续噪声动作序列依赖。轻量上下文压缩器将视觉语言前缀token映射成紧凑条件信号，用于门控和调制各Mamba动作块，避免重复token级跨注意力。动作专家规模缩小10倍；流匹配去噪延迟在LIBERO降低64.8%，真实部署降低58.8%。三个独立微调随机种子下，LIBERO平均成功率提高1.08个百分点，并在LIBERO-Plus和真实机器人基准保持收益。

Insight: 降低反复调用的动作模块成本可提升流匹配VLA的部署效率，同时保留大模型视觉语言条件。摘要未给出总参数量、真实部署延迟绝对值及不同任务成功率，报告的成功率增益幅度较小，需结合方差解读。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have shown strong promise for generalist robotic manipulation, but their inference cost remains a major bottleneck for real-time control. This limitation is especially pronounced in flow-matching VLAs such as $π_{0.5}$, where the action expert is evaluated repeatedly during iterative denoising. We introduce MambaAct, an efficient Mamba action expert for flow-matching VLA policies. MambaAct retains the pretrained visual-language prefix encoder of $π_{0.5}$, while replacing its transformer-based action expert with a compact Mamba-based action expert. Within this expert, Mamba serves as the action mixer that models dependencies across the noisy continuous-action sequence. To preserve visual-language conditioning without repeated token-level cross-attention, MambaAct uses a lightweight Context Compressor to map Vision-language model (VLM) prefix tokens into a compact conditioning signal that gates and modulates each Mamba action block. MambaAct uses a $10\times$ smaller action expert and reduces flow-matching denoising latency by $64.8%$ on LIBERO and $58.8%$ in real-world deployment, while improving mean LIBERO success by $1.08$ percentage points across three independent fine-tuning seeds and maintaining gains on LIBERO-Plus and real-world benchmarks. Our code and model weights are publicly available at https://github.com/giakhuyendihoc/MambaAct.

[131] VocalGrad: Evaluating Acoustic Perception in Audio Language Models cs.SD | cs.CLPDF

Koki Ryu, Hitomi Yanaka

TL;DR: VocalGrad用时间变化方向的二分类任务测量音频语言模型的低层声学感知，发现模型接近随机水平，并定位到信息未被利用或跨层逐渐不可解码两类故障。

Details

Motivation: 大型音频语言模型已处理情绪等副语言和非语言任务，但当声学线索与口语内容共现时，仍难稳定利用声学信息。现有基准未能清楚区分低层声学感知与高层语义推理，难以定位失误来源。

Result: VocalGrad通过判断声学变化方向的简单二分类任务，尽量减少高层推理和语义理解的影响。基准结果显示，人工标注者准确率较高，而先进音频语言模型接近随机水平。线性探针分析揭示两类瓶颈：部分类别所需信息仍保留在内部表示中，但语言模型输出头未能利用；另一些类别中，相关信息随着网络层加深逐渐不再线性可解码。

Insight: 低层声学变化感知可能独立于文本输出中看见的语义能力，单靠问答错误难以诊断。摘要没有给出任务类别、具体模型和准确率，接近随机的结论仅限于受控测试。

Abstract: Recent large audio language models (LALMs) have extended their capabilities beyond lexical speech understanding to tasks requiring paralinguistic and non-linguistic audio understanding, such as emotion recognition. However, these models still struggle to reliably utilize acoustic information, particularly when it co-occurs with spoken linguistic content. Existing benchmarks do not clearly distinguish acoustic perception from higher-level reasoning, making it difficult to identify the source of these failures. To address this issue, we introduce VocalGrad, a benchmark designed to evaluate LALMs’ ability to perceive low-level acoustic variations. VocalGrad formulates this problem as a simple binary classification of temporal change direction, enabling evaluation largely independent of higher-level reasoning or semantic understanding. Our benchmark results reveal a substantial gap in acoustic perception between human annotators and state-of-the-art LALMs: while human annotators achieve high accuracy, LALMs perform at near-chance levels. We further conduct linear probing to identify structural bottlenecks in these models. Our analysis reveals two distinct failure modes: for some categories, task-relevant information is preserved in internal representations but not utilized by the language model head; for others, the information becomes progressively less linearly decodable across model layers. Overall, our results reveal substantial limitations of LALMs in modeling acoustic variation under controlled settings and expose representation differences not observable from text outputs.

[132] TransNSNet: A Transformer-Enhanced Multi-Scale Adversarial Framework for CT-to-PET Image Synthesis eess.IV | cs.CVPDF

Shaojun Li, Xiaoyu Deng, Ikuo Keshi

TL;DR: TransNSNet通过多尺度粗到细监督和带上下文交互的对抗判别器生成PET样式CT图像，取得较好的影像质量趋势，但现有结果未证明统计显著优势或临床有效性。

Details

Motivation: CT到PET合成可提供代谢影像的近似信息，但评估需要严格患者划分及多种图像和患者级指标。单次训练结果或小样本统计不足以支持临床替代结论。

Result: 作者从公开非小细胞肺癌放射基因组数据筛选、切片匹配和预处理，建立患者级训练—测试划分的配对CT-PET数据。TransNSNet在多个空间尺度生成PET样式预测，并进行由粗到细重建监督；TransPatchGAN为对抗评估加入上下文特征交互。渐进消融比较Pix2Pix、NSNet-Base、NSNet-D和TransNSNet，图像级结果取单次训练第80–101轮固定收敛窗口。TransNSNet在十项指标中九项均值最高，但视觉显著性诱导指标由NSNet-Base领先。高强度区域分析中NSNet-D在切片级召回和平衡准确率略高，患者级等权值近似；最高Dice转为TransNSNet。差异相较患者间变异较小，另一个10人自适应患者级分析经Holm校正后无成对显著差异。TransNSNet参数量也明显小于原始next-scale模型。

Insight: 结果支持影像质量改善趋势，但统计分析未证明显著优越性或临床有效性；合成PET样式图不能替代临床采集PET。摘要中的单次训练、10人分析限制了对可重复性和患者级收益的判断。

Abstract: This study investigates CT-to-PET image synthesis using TransNSNet, a Transformer-enhanced multi-scale adversarial framework. A paired CT-PET dataset was curated from the public non-small cell lung cancer (NSCLC) Radiogenomics collection through patient screening, slice-level matching, preprocessing, and patient-level train-test separation. The proposed framework generates PET-like predictions at multiple spatial scales and applies coarse-to-fine reconstruction supervision, while TransPatchGAN introduces contextual feature interaction into adversarial evaluation. A progressive ablation study compares Pix2Pix, NSNet-Base, NSNet-D, and TransNSNet. Image-level performance was summarized over a fixed convergence window (epochs 80-101) from one training run per model. TransNSNet achieved the best mean value in nine of ten metrics, whereas NSNet-Base achieved the highest visual saliency-induced index. For fixed-fraction high-intensity regions, NSNet-D was marginally higher in slice-level recall and balanced accuracy, but the equal-weight patient-level values were nearly identical and the highest Dice values shifted to TransNSNet. These differences were small relative to between-patient variability. In the separate adaptive patient-level analysis (n=10), no pairwise model difference remained statistically significant after Holm correction. TransNSNet also had a substantially smaller parameter footprint than the original next-scale models. These findings indicate promising image-quality trends, but they do not establish statistical superiority or clinical validity; synthesized PET-like images should not be interpreted as substitutes for clinically acquired PET.

[133] Alignment of visual brain encoding models with individual perceptual similarity judgments q-bio.NC | cs.CVPDF

Daniel Chong, Haomiao Chen, Keith W Jamison, Raja Marjieh, Ilia Sucholutsky

TL;DR: 该研究用个体化人类相似性判断微调视觉脑编码模型，发现个体化感知相似度监督比通用群体监督更能对齐模型表征与人类判断。

Details

Motivation: 传统视觉脑编码模型主要通过预测图像诱发的脑活动训练，但内部表征未必反映个体实际感知相似性。研究探索能否把人类相似度判断用于个性化脑编码模型。

Result: 作者使用Natural Scenes Dataset中8名参与者对100张图像的相似性判断，为每位参与者单独适配通用DreamSim模型，得到个体化感知相似性模型，并在各自脑编码训练集上建立个体相似结构。两种条件使用相同初始化和目标，分别以群体通用或个体化相似性微调基线脑编码器。基线编码器反映个体内感知相似性的程度甚至高于测得fMRI响应，但未反映人类相似性判断中的个体间相关结构；加入通用群体监督后，对人类判断的对齐改善，却仍未复现个体间相关性。个体化相似性监督模型最贴近个人判断，并能反映人类判断中的个体间相关结构。

Insight: 将少量个体化行为判断蒸馏进脑编码模型，可能构建更贴近个人感知的视觉表征。摘要样本仅8人、100张图像，个性化模型的稳定性与对更多刺激和参与者的泛化尚需进一步验证。

Abstract: Encoding models that predict brain activation responses to images have traditionally been trained to maximize accuracy when predicting observed brain data. However, their internal representations may not reflect individual human perception. In this work, we investigate whether we can fine-tune brain encoding models to better align their representations with individuals’ similarity judgments. Using similarity judgments of 100 images from 8 participants in the Natural Scenes Dataset (NSD), we adapt a generic human similarity judgment model (DreamSim) separately for each participant to obtain personalized perceptual similarity models. These models are then used to obtain subject-specific similarity structure across images in their encoding model training sets. We fine-tune each participant’s baseline encoder in two conditions, aligning its representations with either generic or personalized similarity judgments, with the same initialization and objective. We find that baseline brain encoders reflect within-subject perceptual similarity more strongly than measured fMRI responses and preserve intersubject correlations present in measured fMRI, but fail to reflect those of human perceptual similarity judgments. Fine-tuning with generic, population-level perceptual similarity information results in better alignment with human judgments than baseline encoders, but these models still fail to capture intersubject correlations of human judgments. However, fine-tuning with personalized perceptual similarity information results in models that are the most aligned with human perceptual similarity judgments and also reflect intersubject correlations of human judgments. Our framework provides an approach for bootstrapping human similarity judgment data into more individualized, human-aligned brain encoding models of vision.

[134] Is a Linear Probe Evidence of a Linear Representation? stat.ML | cs.CV | cs.LGPDF

Nadir Kutluozen, Christian Internò, David Klindt

TL;DR: 该研究指出线性探针高准确率不足以证明视觉表征具有主导性的线性生成因子，并提出反向可预测性、谱集中度和分布外泛化三种诊断。

Details

Motivation: 线性表征假说认为预训练视觉模型把生成因子编码为特征空间中线性可解码方向，常见做法是训练线性探针并以高准确率认定因子已表征。该检验可能过于宽松，无法区分真正线性结构与复杂非线性表征上的简单读出。

Result: 作者说明，渐近条件下足够丰富的特征映射（甚至随机映射）也能容纳线性读出；因此相同探针成绩可能对应两种几何：潜变量对齐子空间占主导的线性世界模型，或潜变量线性嵌在更大曲面流形中的核机器。曲面结构还可能影响分布外泛化、鲁棒性和组合迁移。研究提出三种非对称诊断，并在已知真值的全观测模拟中验证；将方法用于SVG-World上的六个预训练视觉Transformer及CelebA上的两个模型，发现预训练确实产生超过随机核的结构，但远未达到线性世界模型。dSprites、3D Shapes和MPI3D分析也显示正向可解码性与潜变量对齐方差可分离。

Insight: 探针能解码某属性，不代表该属性对应表征的主要、低维几何方向；评估应加入反向预测、谱结构和域外测试。摘要未列出三种诊断的定义公式与阈值，不能仅凭正向准确率推断模型的生成机制。

Abstract: The linear representation hypothesis holds that pretrained vision models encode generative factors as linearly decodable directions in feature space, and comes with a standard methodology: train a linear probe, declare a factor represented if accuracy is high. We show this test is too permissive. Asymptotically, any sufficiently rich feature map, including a random one, admits a linear readout, so high probe accuracy is consistent with two very different geometries: a true linear world model, where the latent-aligned subspace dominates the representation, or a kernel machine, where the latents lie linearly inside a much larger curved manifold. This distinction matters because the curved structure orthogonal to the probe is relevant for out-of-distribution generalization, robustness, and compositional transfer. We propose three asymmetric diagnostics that distinguish the two cases: reverse predictivity, spectral concentration, and out-of-distribution generalization. We validate them in a fully observed simulation where ground truth is known. Applied to six pretrained vision transformers on SVG-World, our deterministic generative benchmark with paired visual styles, and to two of them on CelebA, they show pretraining produces structure beyond a random kernel but well short of a linear world model. On dSprites, 3D Shapes and MPI3D, forward decodability and latent-aligned variance dissociate. Thus, forward decoding alone does not show that the probed factors align with the dominant geometry of the representation. In summary, our work cautions about the success of linear probes as opposed to truly linear and low dimensional representations.
